AGANCHENGANI 


Achikrangni itihasko aganbo inode, Achikrangko Jappa-Jalinpa aro 
Sukpa-Bongepani dilbaachi Tibetoni brangbaani kattarangkosan an-ching 
knaronga. la brangbaani itihasko Jobang D. Marakni Garo History aro 
Dewansing Rongmithuni Apasong Agana aro gipin gipin seanirangoba nikna 
mana. Indiba Tibetoni re-bae Garo Hillsona sokbaani ja-mano daalona 
kingking Achikrangni Atchu Ambini Ma-ambirangko darangba A-chikkuchi sea 
dongkuja. Beben Samson K. Sangmani Geographyo on-tisa Achikni itihaskoba 
seani gnang aro Levisond N. Sangmani Garo Historyoba seani gnang. Kandura 
W. Mominni Assam Kattaoba janapanide gnang indiba iarang chu-sokbegipa 
seanirangde ong-kuja ine nikna man a. Adita Articlerangba A chikni itihasni 
mitam bakrangko seanirangkoba nikna man a indiba uamangni janggi 
tanganiko sulsul see donani da-onaba dongkuja. 

Anga da-o ‘Atchu Ambini Ma-ambi", Britishni Chasong ine kitapni 
bimingko done seaha. Ian A chikrangko Britishrangni chagrongchengaoni Garo 
Hillsko dokkol kae sason kae India jakgitei ong-aona kingking segipa 
A chikrangni itihas ong-a. lako sena gita anga bilsi 30 na bate India, France aro 
Englandoniko ama dipet segitcham aro file gitchamrangoniko segate ra-e 
uarangoniko A-chikkuchi seskaaha. Segate ra-giparang, Filerang aro 
Documentrang inditan bang-a aro bakroani gimin anga uarangoniko kan-dik 
kan-diksa seskaaha. Anga File aro Documentrangoniko pe-joljolaiasani gimin

Sentencerang rough ong-a ine anga an-tangan nikkuenga indiba somoerang 
re-angbeengahani gimin iakon chapa ka-atchengna skaha. 

Mikkangchi Nokgipa angna bil on-kuode anga Britishrangni re-bana 
skang A chikni itihasko kitap kingsa aro India jakgitei ong-ani ja-manoni 
da alona kingking kitap kingsa sena skani gnangchim maina ango adita 
chimonggiminrang gnang. Mai ong-gen ukode u-ikuja. N.E.H.U., Tura 
Campuso L.D.C. ka-enggipa Mr. Talsrang M. Sangma ia pilakkon type ka-e 
on-ahani gimin anga uko mittelbea. Prof. (Mrs.) Caroline R. Marakko cover 
designna photorangko on-anina mittelna ska. 

Meghalaya Sorkarini Arts & Culture Department tangka paisachi 
dakchakanina uamangko mittela. Tura Book Roomko chapa ka-atanina aro 
Eeu Dee Printers-ko ta-raken chapa ka-anina mittelbea. 

lako porae A-chikskarang maiba namgniko man-a ong-ode uan angni 
pilak neng-e seanirang indin ong-gramaia ine anga chanchijawa, batesa angko 
namen didiataniba ong-gen. 

Uni gimin anga ia kitapko poraigipa angni pilak A-chikskarangna 
pakwata.

BAK-1 


BRITISHNI JAK SNILATCHENGANI, 17(5-1815 

1765 bilsini August 12 tariko, Moghulni raja Shah Alam H, 
Bengal, Bihar aro Orissao sason ka-ani aro kajina ra-ani bilko East 
India Companyna on-aha. Ia on-anian, ia damgittam Provincerangni 
pilak a-dokrangko nirokna aro kajinarangko gile ra-na ia Companyna 
bilko man e on-aha. Maming ain gri Bengalko ra-sekon, East India 
Company Indiani Salgro-Salaramni pilak jakgitelgipa aro 
jakgitelsrang ong-gijagipa jatrangko chagrongaha. Uandake Garo 
Hillschi an pae donggipa Indiani Salgro-Salaramni pilak a-dokrangan 
Fort Williamni sasonni ning-o ga-akbaaha. 1 Indiba ua a-dokrangko 
aro uarango songdonggipa jatrangko ma-sikujani gimin, 
Moghulrangni daka gita, Companyba uarangko sasontangona 
ra-bana miksonga dongkujachim. Moghulni kajina giltimgipa officer, 
Fouzdarni pal Company Sezawal minggipa kajina giltimgipa 
officerko rakkiskahaoba Jomindarrang aro uamangni officerrangan 
pilak kajinarangkon gile ra-kuengachim. Kakketko agangenchimode, 
a- song chigatangko nirokanio aro anti-a-kangrangoniko kajina 
gilanioba skang gitan Jomindarrangnan balko donangkuachim. 
A-chikrangni rang-gitik chadrapbaanirangko rikdoatpilna aro 
iarangna Borgondas minggipa sipaini dolko rakkina, Moghulni 
chasongrango gitan apsan bilko Jomindarrangna on*e 
donangaiachim. 2 Sezawalko bilsiprakna rakkiachim jean an-tangni 
bilsi gimiko Companyna on-na nanga tangkarangko on-e ska gita 
retrangoniko Jomindarrang baksa melie gile ra-aiachim. Sezawal aro 
Jomindar, Jomindar aro A-chikrangni gisepo mai meligrikani ba 
meligrikgijanirang gnang, Jomindarrangni a-dakrangoniko badita 
gam man-ani gnang aro a-brini a-dokrangoniko badita man na nanga, 
mamingkon sandia dongjachim. Uarangni gimin, JomindaiTang 
A-chikrangko sinjetangkuaha aro uamangni dokpret-ra-pret 
dakarangko ka-saninggija dake bamataha. 3

nambatatnasa milrpnlcma nie dakanisanchim.

bang-a sipairangko rimbae a doktangtangni ari-simarangni 
sepangjolraogo donggipa a-brini songrangko bobil dinge napaha. 
Uamang bewalan bang-a a-brini ajarangko antangtangni sasonona 
ra-baaha aro A-chikrangkoba drae bamataha. Ramram Choudhury 
adita bang-a A-chik Nokmarangko bamataha jemangan bamani chin 
gitakilchikajina on-naku-rachakaha aro an-tangtang mahari maharini 
gisepo jegrikanirangko bichalna Jomindarkon ra-chakaha. 
Dhruvanarayan bang-bata ajarangko ame man-aha aro an-tangni bil 
gnanganiko ra-chakani chin gita amgimin A-chik Nokmarangko 
kajina on-draataha. Jomindami a-doktangtangni ning-o donggipa 
anti-a-damrangonasan manderangko watatchina gita Nokmarangko 
ge-etataha. 

Dharmanarayan Choudhury (1776-78) jean Dhruvanarayanni 
depante aro Jomindar ong-rikgipa ong-achim, skangna bate rakbate 
biltangko jakkalaha. Uni depante aro Jomindar ong-rikgipa, 
Mahendranarayan Choudhury (1778-1815), ka-saninggijanio aro 
sinjetanio una skanggipa Jomindarrangna batachim. Bang-a 
neng-nikanirang ong-oba, Karaibari aro Mechparani Jomindarrang 
antangtangni dokkol ka-gimin ajarangko aditan bang-a bilsirangna 
ning-o donachim aro bamani chin gita uamangni maniani salrango 
Nokmarangko a-brini bosturangko ra-bae kajina on-rongatachim. 15 
Uni biteara, je gita Moghulni chasongo jakgitelaniko A-chikrang 
man-paachim, jeni gureo gakate dakgrikgipa sipaini dolrang ia napna 
man-gijagipa diltugipa buring-bolgrimrangona napna man-jachim, 
da-o uamang bakbakan gimaataha maina chapchap donggipa 
Jomindarrang British sorkarioniko slairangko bi-aton man-aiachim 
aro uarangna A-chikrangni chri-brarang, mil-amrang, aro sel-urang 
mamingba ong-pajachim. 16

Untaltal, Mechparani Jomindar A-chikrangna ramramde 
namsangchacha chanchie dakbaaha. Indiba Karaibarini Jomindar,

ka-onangatako man e A-chikrang ari-simarango bilchi-jakchi

Mahendranarayan 1798 bilsionin kajina chotkugija dongaengachim.

dongaiaha. British aro Jomindami gisepo dakgrikani A chikrangko

gimin, namgija obostaoniko naljokatna 1809 bilsio Board of

rime, da-ode matnangani dongjaha ine jinmana parakatachim. Iakon 
“Jakgitok Sika” me minga, Unikode uamang dikka te-saonikochu 
ringrimgenchim . 54 Mitam jolrango, a-jak sokna miksongode, Silika 
bolko ge e ua bolni biteko ua bobilni sko baksa cha-gen me 
mikchetkangachim. Chasongni chasongnaba a-jak sokna man-janaba 
donga, indakode ua bobil dingani dedrang-su-drangona 
sokangachim. Choi man-on bobilko sko sote a-jak sokna man-ode 
ge-gimin Silika bolni biteko sotgimin sko baksa chio ritachim. Unikba 
ritgimin bitchiko tom-bagipa pilak manderangnan on-achim. Unikode 
bolko den-e galachim aro bobil dinganiba bon-jokchim . 35 

Uni gimin, Britishrangni re-bana skang chasongrango 
A chikrangni janggi tanganio, a-jak soka aro sko sotani bewalrang 


gapaiachim ine nikatna man-a. Iani a-selko sandiate niode, 
skomangmangnade indakpile ka-niljawachim indiba an-tangtangni 
duk aro onai ka-ako man-ani sal-somoerango darangba kakket d^e 
bichal ka-e on-gipa dongjani gimin indakgipa obostarangona

sokangna nangahakon ine chanchiatna man-a. Jeba ong-bo, British 


sorkari ia bewalko gimaatna rake kam ka-e bon-chotao man -aha.

BAK-2 


DAVID SCOTT ARO UNI KAMRANG, 1816-1831 

Achik aro Jomindarrangni gisepo onga obostarangko name 
sandie sorkarina report onchina David Scottko 1816 bilsio watalanian 
British soikarini A-chikrangna dingtang miksonganiko mesokaha 
aro uan mikkangchi Biistish aro A-chikrangni itihaso gital Bak-ko 
oprakaha. Ia itihasni gital Bak-ko oprake on gipara David Scottan 
ongachim maina ua Indiani Salgro-salaramo donggipa pilak 
adokrangnan sasonni niamrangko bikote see donangaha. Uni gimin, 
David Scottni pilak on gilanganina Alexander Mackenzie indake 
seaha: 

“David Scottni biming aro mingsingani Indiani Salgro-salaramo 
tangsekgilgil dake dongkuenga. Ua mingsingako mangipa 
manderangoni saksa ong achim jean bang a changon Britishrangni

Indiao sason ka aniko nitoatachim. Uni janggi tangao kam ka-ani 
biap Assamni mittin dakgipa buringgrimo onggija Indiani Salgro- 
saliram ba bijangchi jolrango ong genchimode jeon British 
songnokni dal dalbea neng nikanirangko tik kaachim, uaba 
Malcolm, Elphinstone aro Metcalfemang baksa itihaso biap 
mampagenchim.” 1 

David Scottni on-gilarangni gimin sandichengna skang uni 
janggi tanganiko nichengna. Ua Scotlandni Salgro-salaramchi 
donggipa mande rabeako man gipa Scott machongo 1786 ni May 
16 tariko atchiaha. Uni atchutangko Robert Scott mingachim aro 
uan ukgil aro Member of Parliamentba ong-achim. Uo gam 
bang achim. Uni depante saksako Archibald Scott mingachim. 
Archibald Scott man-e-chabegipani demechik Elizabeth Rennysko 
bia ka-ejikni pagipani gamrangko manrikaha. Uoni demechik sakgni 
gnangchim. Uni siani ja-mano Archibald Scott Alexandemi King’s 
Collegeni Principalni demechik Margaret Chalmersko bia ka taiaha 
aro uoni degiparang-Robert Scott, David Scott, Isabella Scott aro 
Anne Scott ong achim. Uandake David, Archibald Scottni gnigipa 
depante ong achim. 

1 . Mackenzie, A., The North-East Frontier of India, P. 5. 




[27] 


David Scott Musselburg Academy minggipa nambegipa 
schoolo poraiaha jeon ua Latin, French, Drawing, Geography, 
Writing, Arithmetic aro Book-Keepingko poraiachim. Ua namgipa 
chatro ong-a ine Academyni Rector certificateko on-aha. 1801 bilsio 
ua East India Companyo clerk ongaha aro 1802 bilsio East India 
Companyni servicena officer seokako man-aha. Indake ua 1802 ni

October 15 tariko Indiaona sokbaaha aro bilsi gnina Calcuttao Fort 
William Collegeo napaha jeon ua chasongo pilak I.C.S. officerrangan 
training ra-na nangachim. 1804 bilsio Hindi aro Persian kusikrango 
ua gnigipa classo pass kaaha. Ia collegeo poraimitingo ua skgni 
inanderang baksa namen ripeng ongaha. Uamangoni saksara 
George Swinton jean ja-mano India sorkarini Chief Secretary ong-e 
David Scottni pilak kam ka-na janapanirangko chu-sokatna 
dakchakbeaha. Sakgipinara William Carey jean Fort William 
Collegeo Bengali ku-sikko skigipa ongachim. David Scottara 
toromigipa mande ongachim aro indaken Carcyni Missionary ni gisik 
uo nape Indiani Salgro-Salaramchi kam kamitingo ua Kristian 
toromko gipatna jotton ka aha aro uandake ua Serampore Collegeona 
A-chik sak 3, Punjabi sak 1, Marathi sak 1, Khasi sak 2, Arakan sak 
2 aro bang-a Bengalirangko poraina watataha. 2 

Fort William Collegeo training matchote David Scottko 
Gorakhpurchi Assistant Collector dake 1804 ni August jao watataha. 
1812 bilsini March jao ua Pumeani Acting Judge aro Magistrate 
ongaha. Apsan bilsini December jao uko Rangpur Districtni Judge 
aro Magistrate dake watataha. 1814 bilsio British-Nepal dakgrikao, 
Bhutan, Sikkim aro Tibet baksa meligrikaniko dake nama bimingko 
man-aha. 1 8 1 6 ni September jao uko Cooch Behami Commissioner 
aro Rangpumi Joint Magistrate dakaha. Ia kamko ka mitingo David 
Scott A-chikrangni bidingo ning-tuen chanchie kam ka-na nangaha. 
Ia a-brini manderangni namgnina rokom rokomrangko agane on-ani 
gimin uarangko kam ka-na gita India sorkari officer saksana kam 
donaha aro ua kamrangko ka-na gita David Scottna bate nambatgipa 
mandeko man jani gimin David Scottkon 1822 bilsio Rangpumi

one watataha. Cooch Behamiba Commissioner ong-angkuaha aro 
unbaksana 1823 ni November 15 tariko uko North-East Frontier of 
Bengalni Governor Generalni Agent dakeba donataha. Ia bang-a 
officerangko ua uni 1831 bilsini August jao siaona kingking 
chalaiangaha. 3 

1815 ni January aro February jarango Achikrang Kalumalupara 
aro Karaibarini a-dokrango donggiparangko nok so- a, mande den-a 
aro ra-seke ra-ako dontongjani gimin David Scottko Garo Hillschi 
napange sandianiko dakchina apsan bilsion watataha. 4 
Golmalrangko bonatna gita Scott A-chik nappretgiparangni kosako 
sipairangko watataha aro A-chikrangni janggi tanganiko neng batatna 
gitapilak andrangko chipaha. British sorkari Scottni Achikrangko 
sasti on-na dakanirangko namnikpaaha aro uko namedake sandie 
nichina ge-etaha. Indiani Governor General, Lord Hastings iarangko 
nichina gita agandapaaha - (1) Bhagalpurni abrini jatrangni 
Clevelandni dingtang niam dakgipa Garo Hillsnaba choligenma, (2) 
Garo Hillso songdonggipa a brini manderangko rimchimonge 
chongipa sipai dolko dake uamangko antidamrango aro a-brichina 
rama dakgiparango donna, (3) Bichal ka-na chu-onge bilko on e 
saksa officerko donkamna, aro (4) A chikrangko gipinrang baksa 
bading-chiwalaniko dakna didina niamko dakna. 5 

Ia sing-anirang aro ge-etanirangna aganchakna gita Scott adita 
jarangna Garo Hillsni an-pagipa bakrango wene ua an-tangni nika 
nianiko ra-chimonge 1816 ni August 20 tariko sorkarina reportko 
on-aha. 

Scott indake nikaha je, Garo Hillschi nangchapgipa Jomindar 
sakbri Achikrangko antangtangna kajina on-e bame dongatna ba 
uamangko an-tangtangni retrang dakna jotton kaahachim. Karaibario 
uni bilakgipa Jomindar an-tangni a-doko songdonggipa bang-a

A-chikrangkon ramram retrang dakataha. Banggija Nokmarangsan 
Jomindami dam dakao kilrangko pale aro Puja somoerango boksis 
on e bame dongaha. Kalumaluparani Jomindar bilgri ong-ani gimin 
uni a-dokrango songdonggipa Achikrang jakgitel gitan ongpilachim.

Jomindami ning-o dongaoniko ma-ekatchina janape on- aha indiba

uamangna a-dok gimaatani pal gita krae on-skaniko dakgen indiba 
A-chikrangni songdonggipa a-jarangko Britishni sasonona 
ra-baskagen jena Nokmarang bamani chin gita bostuchi kajina 
on-skagen. A-brini japarango atgipa anti-a-damrang British soikarini 
ning-o ong-gen aro tom-tome bading-chiwalgen ine songgigrikesa

A-chikrangko uarango bading-chiwalna on-gen. A-chik Nokmarang 
jemangan a-kaweni ning-o a-jong-rorirangko, bosturangko on-ode 
na-simangko chadrapjawa ine ka-mikkenate mal-mattarangko ra-seke 
ra-ronga, uamang uni pal bilsiprako boksisrangko man-gen, 
Jakgitelgipa A-chikrang anti-a-damrangona re-baon uamangoniko 
bang-gija kajinarangko ra-gen, indiba bame donggiparangoniko 
ra-jawa. 

Bhagalpurni a-brini jatrangna Clevelandni 1788 bilsio 
dingtangmancha sasonni bidingo niam dakgipako A-chikrangnaba 
on-na ine chanchion, uade A-chikrangnade nambea ong-jawa ine 
Scott nikaha. Bhagalpurni tribalrango gadangni gadang Nokmarang 
gnang jenan manderang bamachim aro jemangkon a-kawerangchi 
ie-ange dokpret-rapret dakani gimin British sorkari sastirangko one 
bamatna man-ahachim aro anti-a-damrangko chipate uamangko 
kangal ong-ate skimaate, unikoa uamangna tangkachi ba 
bosturangchi on-skaniko dake dokprct-ra-prct dakaniko dontongatna 
man-ahachim. Uandake Nokmarangko maharitangtangko nirokchina 
gita kam ka-atna man-aha. Tosusagrike nie, Scottni ma-sia gita, 
A-chikrang man e dia-kalachim aro indake tangkachi ba bosturangchi 
uamangko ropna man-jachim. Aroba, A-chikni songsaro Nokmako 
sko chakate jinmasa chalaiachim. Uni gimin Nokma namniko ba 
jinma namnikjaode kam ong-jawachim. Uni giminsa A-chikrang 
rasong chabee A-chik a-songko “Ang-ke” baksa tosusaaha jeon sko 
dongjachim. Uni gimin Nokmarang an-tangtangni changa-sapanichi 
ba bilak-wasanichi ba ma-drang jelanichi ba gamsengchi ba jedakeba 
bil dongbo, Scottni nikani gita ua sorkarina indita mangrake 
dakchakna man-gipa ong-jawachim. Gipin a-dokrangko songga- 
gaanio ba a-kingtangko nirokanio Nokma changgipa sapgipa ba 
mingsinggipa ong-naba donga indiba uni dilaniko pilakan ja-rikna 



[31]

nanga ine uamangni Niam-ritio dongjachim. Bil, jean jinmani ong-a 
ine bebera-anio mongsongbate ba chu-gimik pangchaka, ua bil 
jinmani skani ba namnikani gita ong-jaode* ua maming miksonganio 
kamna ostro ong-na man-jawachim. 

Ia obostao, Scottni chanchiani gita Nokmarangkomangmang 
rimbana man e British sorkariko A-chikrangni kosako gatna

songrangkon jerangan Jomindarni ning-o dongachim indiba

dongna nanggen.

jatrangnade cholijawa ine Scott nikmanahachim. Uni gimin ua da-o

Iarang: 

1. British sorkariko ra chakani chin gita uamang kilrangchi 
bilsiprako kajina onna nanggen. 

2. Uamang saksa sakgipinna aro akingtangtangna jamin chakna 
nanggen.

an-tangtangko ra-rikna nanggen, rang-san chagrope 
rasekgiparang uamangni songo katnapon uamangko sorkarini 
jako on na nanggen aro noktangtango sko bikrokrangko sikee 
donani bewalko watna nanggen. 

4. Saniba song gaana am-ako uisoode uko sorkarina nalis ka-na 
nanggen. 

5. Uamang anti-a-damrangona dakgrikani ostrorangko ra-e 
rebajawa, pale man-ani kosako drae ra dapjawa, kraa gita 
damko on-gen aro antidamni mohorina pilak kajia golmalrangko 
pakwatgen aro Magistrateni bichalko manigen.

6. Sorkarini Peskal aro Officerrangko maming jakdangdikgija 
songtangtango napna on na nanggen aro

nokrangko aro mal-mattarangko Magistratechi korok ka-gen. 9

manderangna uamangni sepang jolrango donggipa songrang aro

kanggipa a dokrango songdonggipa Achik Nokmarang Scottko

simsakani dongjawa ineba Scott nikaha. Scott an-tangara

sajinmamitingo tika su-aniko a-bachengataha aroba A-chikrangni

jakgitelgipa A-chikrang baksa mikkangchakgrike grongna nanga

British sorkariko altue rikna man-gen.

donggiparang ong-jani gimin maming namgipa meligrikaniko 
A-chikrang baksa dakna man jawa.

REGULATION X OF 1822 


1. Talatchengani : Bengal Presidencyni ningo dingtang 
dingtang biaprango bang-a jatrang gnangchim, jemangan gipin 
ramram jatrangoni dingtangachim aro jemangnan British Sorkarini 
sasonna dakgimin niamrang cholijawachim. 

Rangpumi Salgro-salaramni ari-simarangoba skia mangijagipa 
ka-namgijagipa jatrang dongachim aro uarangoni mongsonggipa 
A-chik jat ongachim jemangan antangtangchi minggipa a-brirango 
songdong-a-chaachim. la manderangko nambatatna ba soibho 
ong-atna mamingkoba dakkujachim. Uamang aro a-kaweo 
songdonggipa manderangni gisepo bobil ong grikani gimin 
uamangko nangrimatna man jachim, batesa uamangni saknaatgrikani 
uamangko dakgrik-dinggrikatachim aro anchipari onggrikatachim. 
A-chikrangchi an-pagipa Jomindarrangan jakgitelgipa Achikrangni 
aro gipin skia man gijagipa jatrangni a dokrangchi napprctangchenge 
uamangan bobil dingchenggipa ong-a ine bebera-na sakki donga, 
jena mamingkoba gipin cholko dakskana man jae Achikrang sriksrik 
a-jak sokna cholko ra-na draatako manachim. Ia napprete drae 
ra-anirang ruuta bilsirang ong-ahani gimin Jomindarrang 
uamangoniko rokom rokom kajinarangko ra-ahachim aro uarango 
pangchake Land Settlement dakani somoio ia a dokrangko 
Jomindarrangni ning o ga akate Bengalni Regulationni ning o 
ong ataha. Indiba indake dakaniara uamangko soibho ong-atna 
dakchakani pal, uamangko ka-o nangbatataha, maina Bengalni 
sasonni niam iandake chipchange donako man gipa jatrangna

cholijachim aro ian A chikrangni dukrangko namatna dakani pal 
uamangko Jomindami ka-sachakanio donna kam ka-anisa ongachim. 
Adita bilsirangna skangonin ari-simarango donggipa a-brini 
A chikrang aro gipin skia man-gijagipa jatrangni obostarang British 
sorkarini nachil songaniona sokbaaha aro mai a-selrang uamangni 
silrorogniko peng-pangachim uarangkoba sandiman aha aro 
u-iman-aha. Uni gimin indakgipa jatrangko soibho ong-atna kam 
ka-na didina gita, uamangni Nokmarang baksa chanchirime 
uamangko bichal ka-ani niamrangko uamangni ra-chakna namnikani 
gita uamangni niam-ritirango pangchake dakna nanggen gita nika

aro apsan dake uamangko Jomindarrango pangchakaoniko jakgitel

II. Rangpur districtni Goalpara, Dhubri aro Karaibari 
Thanarangni ning-o donggipa pilak a-dokrangkon Rangpur 
districtoniko ra-ongkataha aro Bengalni Regulationrang ia 
a-dokrango cholijawaha. 

IQ. Civil, Criminal, Revenue aro Police aro sorkarini pilak

Departmentrangan Govemor-General-in-Councilni rakkigipa officer 
saksani jako ong-gen, aro ukon Rangpumi Salgro-salaram jolrangni 
Civil Commissioner ine minggen. Ua officeran, Govemor-General- 
in-Councilni dingtang dake ge-etjaskal Bengal Regulationo 
pangchake kam ka-gen. 

IV. (i) Mande doka ba den- a (Criminal) caserango da-o 
Magistrateni bilrango gitan apsan bil man e dos dakgiparangko 
Commissioner bichal ka-na man-gen. 

(ii) Kosako janapbagipa bilrangko jakkalengon, Commissioner 
Bengal Regulationrangko gisik ra-e ka-gen indiba Govemor-General- 
in-Councilni ge-etanirangko man-on ugita kam ka-skagen. 

(iii) Bichalona ra-bagipa dos dakgipako sasti on-na nangani 
Circuitni Judgeni rai on-na man-ani bilna bataha ine Commissioner 
nikode, ua raiko on-na skang uni bichalni serikanirangko Nizamat 
Adalotna on-chenggen. 

(v) Kosako janapbagimin gita Commissionemi watatgipa 
caserangko Nizamat Adalot man-soode ua Law Officerrangna 
pakwatgija name chanchibewale, kraa gitan uan bonkame raiko 
on-gen. 



[44] 


(vi) Achik ba gipin skia mangijagipa ong-gijagiparangni civil 
caserangko jako ra-on, Commissioner dos dakgiparangko bichalona 
okamgen aro Bengal Regulationrangni gita uamangko bichale rai 
on-gen indiba zillani Judge gipinko aganode ugita ja-rikgen. 

(vii) Civil case dolgnian A-chikrang ba ugita apsananggipa

gipin skia man-gijagipa jatrang ong-ode, uamangko bichal ka-anio 
maidake bichal ka-na nanga uko Govemor-General-in-Council 
aganatgen aro ugitasan bichal ka-e raiko on-gen aro uan Kacharini 
rai on-a gita apsan ong-gen. 

(viii) (a) Kajina ra-ani bidingo Commissioner Bengal 
Regulationrango pangchake kajinako gilgen indiba Govemor- 
General-in-Council basakoba maiba dingtang niamko ja-rikchina 
ge-etode ugita dakgen. 

(b) Commissionemi ning-o donggipa a-dokrangni je 
casekoba gipin Civil Courtrango bichal ka-na man-jawa. 

(ix) Section II-o janapbagipa a-dokrango donggipa A-chikrangni 
aro gipin apsananggipa skia man gijagipa jatrangni pilak 
caserangkon, bichalna gita maiba chu-onggijani donggenchimode 
uko Govemor-General-in-Councilna pakwatgen jeni Secretary ua 
caseo maikai dakna nanga uko see u-iatgen. 37

Ua Achikrangna name kam ka ahani gimin sorkarioniko nama 
mittelaniko man-aha. A chikrangna kam ka anio ka sae dakani aro 
ripeng dakanimangmang uni ostrorang ongjachim. Scottni aganani 
gita, Achikrang gipinna dosko dakode uko kasne agane one dosko 
masiatna manchongmotjawachim; ukode silchidare ba slairangko 
mesoke a-jak sokna am osa ma-siatna man aigenchim. Aganode 
indake aganna man-gen je, A chik mandeko mamingsaloba dosni 
a-selko agananiko knachakatna man jawachim, jena kingking uko 
doke aganku jachim; unosa ua slaini gatongko ku-dime an-tangna 
bil gnangbata ine ua mandeko knachakgenchim. A chikrang Scottko 
batanggimin bilsi 7 ba 8 rangna an-tangtangni pagipa ine 
agantokachim indiba ia ka saani kattako ua pagipani rake sason 
kaanichisa manahachim. Sipairangni aro slairangni bilo kadonge, 
Scott sak 20 ba 30 sipairangko rime uamangni diltugipa 
abimandurangchi dos dakgiparangko sasti on-nareangna jajajachim 
aro pangnan chu sokgipaba ong rongachim. Changsao ua sak 10 ba 
12 sipairang baksa sak 700 ba 800 matgrikrangchi changdulako 
man-ahachim, aro indaken uko sal 7 ba sal 8 rangna chipchangaha- 
chim indiba gipin British sipairang re-baesa uko jokataha.

BRITISHNI A'BENGRANGKO GRO DINGANI 

Jena kingkin g David Scott an-tangan A-chikrangna simsakna 
man-achim, A-chikrangni songdonggipa ari-simarango tom-tomani 
donga gita nikachim aro pilak abrini jatrangni gisepo A-chikrangan 
Britishni sasonko ra chakchenggipa jat ong-gen gita nikachim. Indiba 
British aro Burmeserangni gisepo Assamo dakgrik-dinggrikani 
ong-on, Scottko A-chikrangna kam ka-aoniko okamange uko Indiani 
Salgro-Salaram jol gimikna Govemor-Generalni Agent dakate 
1823 ni November 20 tariko watatskaaha. 1 Uni biapko Goalparani 
Principal Assistant ra-rikskaachim aro Sarbarakar (Indian Officer) 
saksa officer dakchakachim aro jeni skotonggipa biap Singimari 
ong-achim. Iani ja-mano British sorkari ruuta bilsirangna a brini 
A-chikrangni bidingo mamingba jakdangdikgija dongaiachim. 
A-brini ja-retrango songdonggipa A-chikrang ku-rachakatang gita 
ka-jinarangko tik ong-e on-jachim. Batesa rang-san chagrope 
ra-seksimsimaiachim jeni gimin British sorkari sipairangko watate 
sasti onrongachim ba anti-a-kangrangko cha-na on-jachim. Basakoba 
basakoba Goalparani Principal Assistantni songrangona re-ange 
grongode golmalrang namkalachim indiba je bilsirango songree 
Scottni daksamsoani gita tik ong-e dakja aro badita ruute dontonga 
inditan golmal-kajiarang bariroroachim. 2 Uni gimin jensalo golmal- 
gondokol ong-a unosa golmal ka-gipa maharirang baksa gital 
songgigrikaniko dakaiachim. 3

1822-23 bilsirango British sorkari A bengrangko kajina 196 
Naraini tangkarangko dabichengaha jensalo A-beng adokrang British 
sorkarini jako gaakaha. Una skangde Kalumaluparani Jomindar 
uamangni kosako bil jakkalachim. Jomindami biloni naljokatako 
man e A bengrang skatang dakna a bachengtaiaha. Uni gimin British 
sorkari 1824-25 bilsirango kajinako 200 Naraini tangkarangona 
bariataha jean Karaibari Jomindami samtangtango aro gipin bakrango 
songdonggipa Achikrangko bobil dake chakatattaiaha.

darangnaba bamgija skatang dakengahani gimin uamangko

sorkarini dao dabigipa kajinarangna uamang maming daito dongjaha 
meba uamang aganaha. Sandie nion, bang-a manderangan 
gammantokahachim ine nikaha indiba uamangko sorkarini 
rongdikona sokatangjaha. Indake kajina gilani niamara namgipa 
ongjachim ine nike, ua bilsi aro una skanggipa bilsini kajinako rae 
una skanggipa bilsini kajinako watode nambatgen ine chanchie 
uarangko wataha. Adita jechakanirangko dake A chikrang ua 
bilsignini kajina aro Rongtupara songko Rs. 1000/- dai dakgipako 
gamaha. Unikoa, Lieut.Brodie mikkangchi Nokmatangtango 
onggija kajinarangko Mongring Laskerona sokatokna nanga ine

aro uamangko British sorkarini sasonni ning-ona ra-bana draana 
nanga, maina uni chanchia gita, A-chikrangko uamangni namgijagipa 
dakbewalrangoniko aro uamangni Nokmarangoniko naljokatjaskal, 
sorkan A-chik jat gimikna namgniko dakna man-jawa. Iako dakna 
man-janaba donga indiba British soricari mamingbajakdangdikjaode, 
uamang mamingsaloba skia man-gipa jatrangni janggi tangani bewal 
gita tangna man-jawa ine raken Govemor-Generalna see watataha . 12 
la seanirangba British sorkarini re-mikkangna miksongani bewalko 
dingtangatjaha. Adita manderangni dos dakana chagrope sasti on-na 
am-ode, mahari gimikkon a-jak sokna draatako man-gen ine soricari 
chanchiaha. Davidsonni report gita. Chenga sardar aro uni 
nokdangko bichal ka-o aro so-oto Chetsigiri aro Rongtupara songni 
me-sasa songdonggipa gimikan dongpaachim. Uni gimin sasti on-ode 
songgnikon on-na nanggen. Beben British sorkari songrangko so-na, 
songni manderangko so-otna, uamangko koedi dake rim-angna ba 
dai gamatna man-gen, indiba iako militaryko rimbaesa man-gen jean 
daiko man-ana bate sorkarina koros ong batgenchim. Aro dos 
dakgiparang baksa dos grigiparangba saknaako man-naba

Senni ku-sikoniko Singimariona sokarin knaaha. Uni gimin ia

chanchiaha. Jeba ong-bo ua Bhogamara songona re angaha aro anna

Magistrateni mikkangona rebatokaha indiba uamang pilakan anna

Nokma sakprakprakan dakchakaniko on-na nanga ga-akachim.

uko grongtainabeaha ine ka-mikkenate agane watataha.

kulirangko A-chikrangni chadrapaoniko chelchakna gita chu-onge 
nitimgiparangko donaha. Apsan attamon, mitam Nokmarang re-baaha 
aro dakchakaniko on-aha. Pring gipino, adita gipin Nokmarang 
dakchakeaha aro indake bang-a kulirangko chimongaha. Gittamgipa 
salo, anna dokni bangki sordarrang, Rampani songni sordarko

man-gope, bang-a kulirangko rimbae buring o-na re batokaha aro je 
nanganioba dakchakaniko on-na aro Rampani songo wa-a ritchok 
su-e guare kanaba ku-rachakaha. Brigipa salo uamang Chakapara 
songona sokeaha jean anna dokni Mahalni skanggipa song 
ong-achim aro sordar saksamangba dakchakna jechakjaha ine nike 
kusi ong-aha. 23

“Bhogamaraoni Bangalkataona dakgipa sorok gimikari 
ongnasipile atimborangni kosak gita dakachim aro unija-retrangode

a-brirangni gisepogipa biaprangko, a a gamna manatpilgija 
chiblokrang pilataha.” 25

man-jawachim. A bri gita dakgipa ramaba adita re-on gitcham ramao 
naeachim, unikode chibimani rikam joljol Rongtuparaona 
dakangachim. Rongtupara songara nambegipa song ong-achim jean

sak 1500 mang jelachim. Rongtuparani Nokma Monkual ong-achim,

a-dokrango silchi-darerangko ra-e apsan jinma re-jome mesokaha, 
Jomindarrangna kajina on-jachina beng-aha aro Bhai Saheb aro Ma 
Sahebani bimingo tangka giltokaha. Jagnimangna kingking uamang 
jekon Pagol ba Pagla manderang ine mingkalakachim, Mymensingni 
simarango dakgrik-dokgrik, dokpret-ra-sek dakaha. 
Burmeserangming dakgrikani gimin British sorkari uamangni kosako 
mamingkoba dakna man-ningjaha. Indiba bonkamao, dakgrikgipa 
sipairangko watataha aro 1825 ni March jaode uamangko jrip 
dakataha. Ia golmalrangko ongatanichi Tiphu mangmang namgniko 
man ahachim maina ua an-tangna Rajani dongram nokko rike 
ra-rikna man aha jekon ua ‘Tiphu Rajani bichal ka-ram Rajani Nok” 
ine mingachim. Indiba bon kamao British sipairang aro Policerang 
cheaha, aro Tiphu aro uni ja-rikgiparangko Patoko chipataha. 

Indake ong-omangba, British sorkari indake u-iaha je 
jenakingking Jomindarrang aro game cha-giparangni da-o 
jegrikanirangko namatna man ja aro game cha-giparangni man na 
nanggnirangko tik ka-e on-ja, ua jolrango golmal-gondokolrangko 
champengna man-jawa. Raitrangni manna nanga ine dabianirangko 
biapona re-ange sandichina gita British sorkari H. Dampair, 
Mymensingni Joint Magistrateko 1825 ni December jao watataha. 
Dampair name sandiani ja-mano indake tik ka-angna manaha je pilak

a-arangan, A-chikrangni gamgiparang ong-ja maina ba gipinrangni 
gamgipa ong-ja maina, Britishni chelchakani ning-o donge game 
cha-gipa pilakan bilsio Rs. 700/-ko British sorkarina kajina on-na 
nanggen, aro Jomindarrang mikkangchi baridapatna man-jawaha. 
Ia tik ka-giminko dolgnioni jeba dingtangatna man-jawa ine 
aganskaaha. Ia tik ka-giminrangko see-jote, soi ka-grike tribal

Goalparani Sub-Assistant John Strong Rajaballa antichi re-angon 
Achikrang aro Bangal badinggiparangni jegrikengako nikeaha.

dongaiani gimin jekon Narainee rupee ine minga (Koch Behar rajani 
dakgipa tangka) uko badinggiparangna on- aha indiba badinggiparang 
rana jechakaha maina sorkarini gital silni tangkako 
jakkalatskahachim. Ia obostako nike, John Strong A-chikrangoniko 
gitcham tangkakon ra china indiba kilrangko sorkarini gital silni 
tangkachi brechina gita badinggiparangko ge etaha. Uandake 
A chikrang merong aro pilak gipin bosturangko gitcham tangkachi 
bretokaha indiba kilrangko gital tangkana palna jechakaha aro 
kiltangtangko ole noktangtangchina re angpiltokaiaha.

Badinggiparangoba gitcham tangka dongskajachim. A chikrang gital 
tangkako rachongmotjawa ine nike, uamangko ra na draode

John Strong masiaha. Achikrangni indakpile ka-a ding doako nike 
badinggiparangba mikkangchi re-bataijawaha ine aganaha. Putimari 
aro gipin gipin antirangoba apsan obosta ongaha. 22 Mr. T. Brodie,

ma-siaha. 53

salbana sorkari kragipa cholrangko dakna cholnikjaha. Draanichi 
aro nangrimgrikpilanichi kam ka-gipa bewai A-chikrangna cholijaha 
Indakgipa kam ka-ani bewalko England, Gladstone aro Disraelin: 
chasongrango Irelandni kosako jakkalahachim jekon “Gatingaaro 
Ku-dima” policy ine mingachim. David Scott iakon ra-gataha. Ukc 
ja-chongrikgipa, T.C. Robertsonba rinok rinok dakachi arc 
meligrikpilachi kam ka-achim. Uni ja-mano Colonel Jenkins, 
chonchongipa officerrangni jakchi A chikrangko rake sason ka-na 
am-achim. Goalparani Principal Assistantrang, jekae, Davidson,

GOVERNOR-GENERAL LORD DALHOUSIENI 
CHANCHISAMSOANI : 

Lord Dalhousie jean skango Achikrangni kosako rake 
sinjetaniko namnikjachim, 8 da-ode ia ong-anggimin a-selrangni gimin 
raken sinjete kam ka-ani kattako aganaha. 1852 ni November 1 1 ni 
Governor-Generalni tom-bimongao mikkangchi kam kana 
chanchisamsoanirangko indake serikahachim : 

1. Skangode indake chadrapanirangna A chikrangni 
songrangko saja on-na aro ge-a gamarangko nosto ka na sorkari 
janapode anga namnikjachim indiba da-ode indake dakna nanggnok. 
Uni gimin mikkangchi sorkarini kam ka-ani ja-pang niamara 
uamangko sorkarina bamatna draani, tom-tomaniko maniani aro 
sorkarini retrangni mal-mattarangko chelchakani ong-gen. 

2. Seanirangchi songgigrikanirang ia manderangni 
dakmikanisan ong-aia. Uni gimin ia chango maming songgigrikaniko 
dakjawa. Ku-rachakatangko chu-sokatjana kingking mandeko 
rim-angoba see-jote songgigrikani gitan namgni dongjawa. A-chik 
panterangna lekka skiade namgen aro uko dakna nanga. 

3. Dos dakgipa songrangchina sipairangko watatna nanga, dai 
gamatna nanga aro mitam Nokmarangko ba uamangni dedrangko 
ku-rachakatangko chu-sokatkujana kingking Goalparachi rimange 
donna nanga. Iarangko dakjaode uamangni gama-ge-anirangko 
nisiatgen ine mikrakatsona nanga. Mikrakatanikoba mingjaode 
kamko chu-sokatna nanga. Dos daktaiode, dontongkujana kingking 
rake sinjetanirangko dakgen. 

4. Indakgipa raka sinjetanirangko sorkari dakna sikja indiba 
sorkarini retrangko so-otna chol on-anpalde uamangko rake sinjetan 
nambatkuenga. 

5. Sorkarini miksongani, uamangna bamna nanga aro

manderang kena gri dongna man na nanga. 

6. Janapgimin gita, namgipa sorokko cho-na man-ode aro Garo 
Hillsko a-a tona man-ode, iarangko dakna kraa. 9 

Uni gimin skango India sorkarini A chikrangni kosako nom-e 
sason ka-aoni da-o Lord Dalbousieni chanchianirang dingtangbeaha 
ine nikgen. Da-o A-chikrangko rake sinjetna nanga ine miksonge 
serikaniko nikaha.

cha anirangkoba raangaha. December 20 tariko Chanangpara 
songona re ange apsan salon sokeaha aro waltuaha. 10 Salgipino 
uamang Garwanggiri songona soke, Mansangiri songona rama 
chona abachengaha, indiba chona abachengon, Achikrang kam 
kaenggipa dolko chadrapaha. Achikrangko a rikatpilna man aha 
aro indake pringgipino Garwanggiri songo napon, songko wate 
katangtokaha ine nikaha. Rebapilchina kobor onattaitaiachim indiba 
darangba mikkang pa ejaha aro Agnew darang baksan agangrikna 
chol ongjaha. Bonkamo songgimikkon aro mi jamrangkoba so toke 
donbaaha. 11

aro Achikrangni gisepo nangrimgrikani namgijaoni namjabataha.

dingani ching chabataha ine Jenkins uiataha. A dokrangni sin a

inamingkon dakna man bajaha. Uni gimin, A chikrang ka sne

a-kawe baksa pilak bading-chiwalanirangko raken champengsrangna 
nanga ine Lt. Agnew sorkarina janape on aha. 12

Assam Light Infantryonikon man gen ine Beckett de e mesoke 
aganaha. Singimario aro uni sepang jolrango songdonggipa Burmese

ba Shan ba Man jatrangni sorkarini dakchakchina agana changantin 
dakchakanina, uamangni ka-donganina aro biaprangko u-ie dormaha 
gri dakchakrongani giminba uamangko mittele Beckett aganaha.

ra chinaba Beckett sorkarina janape on-aha. 47

BAK-6 


GARO HILLSKO RA A, 1860 - 1869 

Pilnipil golmal-gondokolrang aro urangni ari-simarango

kenchipchipatanirang, maiba mingsako dakna kimkim ong e tik 
ka-na India Sorkariko draataha. Ia Achikrangni golmal- 
gondokolrangko namatna gita rakbata miksonganiko Bengalni 
Lieutenant-Governor, John Peter Grant mesokaha. Sarbarakarko 
kamo rakkipiltaichina gita ua 1860 ni March jao namnike rachakaha. 
Obostarangko sandichina aro golmalrangko namatkamna janape 
onatchina gitaba ua Assamni Commissioner, Francis Jenkinsko April 
jao aganataha. 1 Ari-simajoljol wenange obostarangko sandiechina 
gitaba Bengalni Lieut-Governor, Goalparani Principal Assistant, 
Capt. B.W.D. Mortonko ge etataha. Anti-a kangrangoniko 
Jomindarrang kajinarangko gildrae ra kuenga aro ruutkugijagipa 
salrango golmal ka-gipa A chikrangara ua songrangoni ong-a 
jerangkon Susungni Jomindar, Pran Krishna Singh sason manjachim 
mdioba jerangonikon ua ka-jina dabidraachim ine Goalparani 
Principal Assistant, uni Mymensingchipak wenroroango nikeaha. 
Achikrangko rake rim-kepna miksonga gnange, British Sorkarini 
golmal ka-gipa A chikrangko rimna am-aoniko champengna ua 
Jomindar bil ama dipet krengaha. A chikrangni ong-a obostarangko 
Sorkariko u-iatjana ua kam ka-achim maina ua indakarangoniko 
mdake ba uandakeba antangna lap dake ra-rongachim. 2 European 
officer saksa man-a dipet jem-jeman Nokmarangko grongna nanga 
aro ari-sima jolgimikon silchidare baksa Policerangko donna 
nangchongmota ine Capt. Morton chanchiaha. Re-baenggipa 
sinkarini sal-somoerango dolgni sipairangko, golmal ka-giparangko 
rim na gita apsan somoeo Mymensing aro Goalparaoni watatna nanga 
aro uamangni bamaniko mansojaode, bamna sikgijagipasongrangko 
so-na aro uamangni gama-ge arangko nisiattokna nanga ineba ua 
chanchiaha. 3

Achikrangni janggi tangani bewalko nie Jenkins Achikrangko

Sorkarina bilsio kajina game donggiparang; aro 

3. Bemalluahba Jakgitelgiparang.

songrangkon antidamrangona kilrangko ra-baon kajina gilode ia bak 
dakani bon-gnok aro pilak songrangan British Sorkarini ning o 
gaakgnokchim. Ia dingtang tarisoaniko Achikrang chu-ongnikgen

Vernacular Normal skulo porae matchotaha. Uni gimin skul nama 
ge-sako Sub-Assistantni skotong biapo kuliode uamangni poraianio 
bang-a neng-nikanirangko gelna man-genchim.

Act XXII of 1869 : Iakon Garo Hills Act, 1869 ine minga. Ia 
Actni gita Garo Hills Districtni Salgro aro Saliramchi Goalpara 
District, Salgipengchi Mymensing aro Salaramchi Khasi Hills ong-a. 
Bengalni Civil aro Criminal Courtrang aro Revenueni niamrang Garo 
Hillso bil dongjawa. Aroba Govemor-Generalni Councilni pilak Act 
pass ka-giparangko aro niam dakgiparangko Garo Hillsni je bakoba 
ra gatjawa. Civil aro Criminal Justice aro kajinarangko gilani aro 
nirokaniko Lieutemant-Govemomi donatgipa officemi jakchisakam 
ka-gen. Ia donatgipa officer, uni pilak sasonni aro nirokani kamrango 
Lieutenant-Govemomi ge eta gita uni ning-o donge kam ka-gen. Je

mandekon donatgipa officer ba Court Patokona galonaha ba sagal 
nalsachi watatna rai on-aha, uko Lieutenant-Govemomi ge-eton 
dakgen. Lieutenant-Governor kranikode, Jomindarrangni Garo 
Hillsoniko kajina ba gipin gilanirangko champengna bil donggen, 
aro uarangko watnaba mangen ba officertangni jakchi gilate 
Jomindarrangna tangka on skana man gen. Apsan Lieutenant- 
Govemoran, Britishni gipin a-dokrangna dakgimin niamko ba uni 
bakko Garo Hillso, Calcutta Gazetteo seatchenge, ra-gatna man-gen. 
Ari-simarangni bidingo jegrikani ong-ode, Lieutenant-Governor 
officerko donate kam ka-atgen jeni tik ka anian bon-chotgipa tik 
ka-ani ong-gen. 

Ia gitalgipa Act Jomindarrangni kosako ong-aha ine nikna 
man gen. Ian uamangni a-brini manderangoniko kajinarangko 
gilaniko champengaha. Garo Hills Districtni ari-simarangkoba tik 
ka-aha jean Jomindarrangni Garo Hills Districto bilrangko 
bon-atahachim. 

Skangonin uni a-dokni adita bakrangkon gipinchi man-angaha

Secretary of Statena apil ka-aha 69 , indiba knachakjaha, indioba ia 
gital Act ko ra-gatanichi Jomindarrang noksan ong-ahaode uamangna 
jaksrame tangka gamskachina aganataha. 70 Jomindarrangni

BRITISH SORKARIKO A'CHIKRANGNI 
BON KAMGIPA WARACHAKANIRANG, 1870-1873 

1872 bilsini Garo Hills Districtni Administrative Report gita, 
Capt. Williamsonni Garo Hills Districtni sasonko jako ra-chengaona, 
A-chikrangan pilak golmal-gondokolrangkon ong-atachim, aro 
chengo David Scottni chasongonin, bilsisaba mande skona 
akawerangchi chagropgija dongjachim, je somoerangon uamang 
chagropa changantin akaweni manderangni sko dot 5 ba 6 ba una 
baten sote ra angachim. Ningni a-brirangko pilakchin Britishni 
retrang songdongduulachim aro uan apaljaoba uarangchi darang 
Officerba re-angkujachim aro anti-adamrango nikana agre uamang 
unon an-tangtangko chipchange dongaiachim. Capt. Williamsonni 
skanide, ua diltugipa a-bimandurango donggipa A-chikrangko name 
aro tom-tome dongatna aro uamangni songrangchi re-e-doc nina 
ong-achim. Indioba Capt. Williamsonni ia a-brirangona re-bae sason 
ka-chengaonin a kawerangchi chagropangani changsaba 
dongjahachim aro indake a-bri ja-retrango songdonggiparang maming 
kena gri dongna man-aha. 1870 bilsio Captain Williamsonni 
songtangchi chutti ra-e re-angna skangan song ritchasana baten una 
bameahachim aro uni ja-mano D.C. ong-rikgipa Captain La Touche 
na bang-a songrangan re-bae bamedapaha. Capt. La Touche ama 
dipet ning ni songrangkoba re-ange nieaha indiba ning ni bang-a 
diltugipa songrangan jakgi tele dongtokkuengachim. 1

1 870-7 1-ni Reporto Cooch Beharni Commissioner indake 
aganaha, “Chingni Garo Hillsko sason ka-ani biteko nikna 
a-bachengaha maina bilsini gisepo Garo Hillso mande so-otako 
knajaha. Chingni re-bana skangde bilsio sko dot 20 na agrede 
komirongjachim. Uni gimin ia ka-namgija a-song gimikkon chingni 
sasonona ra-bana man-gen ine kusi ong-aha. Ian uamangna aro 
a kaweni manderangna namgni ong-gen. Batanggimin bilsi brini 
gisepo slai pongsaba gojaha aro changsaba a-kawechi sko sotna

Garo Hillsko Survey ka*a, 1870 : 

1870 bilsio Khasi Hillsko survey ka a matchotnasion. Major 
Godwin- Austen aro uni Survey party an tangtangko Garo Hillsni 
salgro-salaramchi sokbaaha ine nikaha aro uamang sorkarina 
bamgipa songrangko jerangan Mymensing aro Goalpara a-kaweni 
sepangrango dongachim uarangona reangna miksongaha. Capt. 
Williamson Major Austenko Mymensingni Nazirpuro 1870 ni 
February jao grongcaha aro ia Officer sakgni somoe man-mitingon 
mana dipet tarake jakgilelgipa A chik songrangchi re angna tik

kaaha. Uamang Susongoni Damraona a briko re sotbaaha aro 
rebamitingo dal begipa aro jakgilelgipa Saramphang songo 
nappakbaaha. Ian skanggipa bigil gipokrangni Mymensingoni 
Goalparaona Garo Hillsko re sotchenggipa ongachim. Batsote 
re bamitingo uamangko Banganggiri song warachaksoahachim 
indiba bobil dinggrikgija melie-nangrime dongna ine agane 
uamangko meliatna manaha. Iako nike gipin jakgitelgipa songrangba 
bobil dingsogija ia survey dolko rimchaksoaha aro bonkamao, 
nokprako kajinako on e British sorkarina bamaniko dakaha. 
A chikrangni Survey partyna a brirangko o time pilak 
dakchakanirangko nike. Survey party kusi ong-beaha aro ian 
chusokbegipa songreani ine nikaha. Garo Hills gimikko survey 
ka ode bang a namgnirang donggen ine nike aro 1870 bilsio 
re sotbagipa inditan sorkariko didiatahani gimin sorkari survey 
ka aniko dakangkuna tik ka aha. 3

Paromgre Songo Napa, June 1872 : 

Paromgre aro gipin gipin jakgitelgipa A-chik songrangko 
bon-kimitatsrangna ba uamangko British sorkarini ning-ona 
ra-basrangna ine India sorkari namnikaha. Uandaken India sorkari 
Garo Hillsni D.C., Capt. Williamsonko ia kamko chu-sokatchina 
ge-etaha. Ua gita, D.C. ba June jani a-bachengaoni, sal-somoerang 
namangjaoba donangchaode nambatrorojawa ine nike 1 872 ni June 
jao ua songrangchi Police dolsako rime re-angaha. 

Goalparaoni reserve Policerang Turaona 1 872 ni June 18 tariko 
sokbaon, D.C. uamangna salsana neng-takna on e, uamang June 20 
tariko Paromgreona Dalu gita re-chakatangaha. Skanggipa salo 
uamang ja-achi mile 1 8 re-ange Keraparaona sokeaha. Gnigipa salo 
mile 20 re-e Daluona sokaha, gittamgipa salo mile 24 re-e Ghosegaon 
songona aro brigipa salo Durgapurona sokaha. Ian neng-begipa re-ani 
ong-achim maina sal namen raka aro banoba banobade ja-sku 
ong-pilgipa a-dubek aro chirangko re-na nangachim. Durgapuro 
uamang sal 3 neng-takaha aro uno neng-takmitingo samtangtangni 
Laskerrangko an-tang baksa re-pachina okamatokaha aro cha-a 
ringanirangko chimongchina ge-etaha. Chiputni Laskerrang 
jemangkon Tobang aro Lobang mingachim, uamang rettangtangko 
rime Juneni 24 tariko sokbaaha. Rakhee, Durga, Sookur aro gipin

Uamang 1872 ni June 27, Bristibar salo Durgapuroni Emangreona 
Simsang chijoljol chonchongipa ringrango chodoange salgipino 
neng-edike sokeaha. 29 tank Sonibar salo uamang Paromgre songona

sokena ka dongachim indiba Emangreni manderang Policerangna 
rationrangko olna re-baningjani gimin ja-manchakaha. Sorkarina 
chakgipa Laskerrangni aganani gita, Chadraptaianirangde dongde 
dongjahachim aro policerangko warachaksona tarisoanirangba 
dongjachim indiba mande so-otgiparangko rim-nade sorkariko bobil 
dakgipa songrangchide re-angna pa-kujachim. Uni gimin iako dakna 
ta-rakna nanggen ine Capt. Williamson nikaha, maina ruutode A chik 
re-chappagiparang un baksa dongchajawa aro sal-somoerangba 
wachi kari abachengbaengahani gimin ua a-brini bakrango 
wenrorona namroroangjawa ine ua kenchakahachim. Uni gimin 
Capt. Williamson salgipinon Bawegre aro Kakwagre songrangko, 
jerangan re-anggipa chango Damukchigiri jolrango mande sootanio 
mongsonggipabakko ra-ahachim aro so-otgipa manderangko donnue 
donengachim, uamangchi re-ange uamangna jagokaniko onna aro 
man-ode nangenggipa manderangko rim-na aro songni manderangko 
sasti onna miksongalia.

Capt. Williamson aro uni police dol Paromgreoni Turaona 1872 
ni July 3 tariko re-chakatbaaha. Re-bamitingo Darranggiri songo 
napbaaha maina ua song Capt. La Touchena kajina on-ahaoba golmal 
ka-taengachim. Sorkarina bamna sikgijagipa bang-a A-chikrangan 
ia songo ma-chakenga ine knae uni re baniko champengsona 
krengtokenga ine Capt. Williamson knasoaha. Ia songan Dak 
ra-ruragipa Police dolkoba chadrapahachim. Capt. Williamson aro 
uni dol ia songona mikka jimako jima minggija konta dok mangna 
baten re-bae 2 p.m. o sokeaha. Mikka bilongen jimdapahani gimin 
Capt. Williamson slai gopongate nioba adha mangde gojahachim. 
Turaona sokbapile Policerangko parade ka-ate slaitangtangko 
gopongate niatoba guli rang 34 oni rong 13 mangsan goaiahachim. 
Uamangni re-bagipa rama a-briona chiringjoljol dakachim aro da-on 
da-on kangkare ong-pilgipa tu-gipa chirangko bate re-na nangachim. 
Uamangko ru-atrangba chiktimole duk on-achim. Darranggiriona 
mile samang dongaonin ramarango wa-chol chaktokachim aro 
indaken saksa sakgni Policerangko bu-aha. Songni ka-mao chiring 
dilsa dongachim aro uko batsotgipa gitan wa-chol chakachim. Mikka 
jime wahani gimin chi dal e chiringo chakgipa wa-cholrangko 
nikjaha. Police Inspector saksa aro sak 12 oni sak 6 mikkango rc-gipa 
Policerang rang-san ning-tue bu-ako man-tokaha aro Capt. 
Williamsonba wa-cholko re-pretman-ahachim indiba ua juta bootko 
ganani giminsa mata bu-ako man-jaha. Uamang man-a dipet ta-raken 
songo napahaoba songni manderang songni ketchi samsa gita 
ta-raken katbildangangtokskaaha. Adita slairangchi gopongatahachim 
indiba darangba an-pilbajaha. Uamangko rikange rim-enaba bilni 
nalsao ong-achim. An-tangtangni katna dongipa ramana agre pilak 
gipin ramarangkon uamang wa-chol chaktokachim. Uni gimin 
uamang altuen katc jokna man-aiachim. Uamangko rikenggipa 
ja-araragipa ramram Policerangde wa-chol chakgipa ramarangko 
re-angna nangani gimin A-chikrangde an-tangtangni tarigimin 
ramarang gita altuen kate jokna man-aiachim. Mikka jimbeengachim

aro chiringrang joksuakpile ta-raken jokengachim. Uni gimin 
A-chikrangko rim-na doanganide ka-donggramaianisa

ong-aigenchim. Indiba Dak ra-ruragipa Police dolko chagropna 
am-simsimaiani gimin aro bobil dingako watgalkujani gimin 
uamangko sasti on-na nangnikchong mote, songgimikkon 
so-srangaha. Unomiting songni manderang gimikan songo 
dongjachim. Uamang a-batangtangchina katdoangtokahachim indiba 
uanoniba uamang Dak ra-giparangko nirikkie dongtokaengachim. 
Nokrang a-kimbrio ong ani gimin ramako nikonataiachim indiba 
uamangko nidoatode nikatskajachim. Uni gimin ia bamna 
sikchomgmotgijagipa songko jekon raka skianiko on-na 
nangnikachim, sasti on-na gita maming chol grichim. Nok so-oba 
sasti on-a gitan dakjachim maina uamang a-bani nokrango dongskana 
man-aiachim. Mi jamrangkoara so-skana sikjacbim. Indiba 
mikkangchi golmal ka-taiode uamangni a-barangko nosto ka-e galna 
miksonge donbaiaha. 

Gipin Songrango napa :

doneako uaba namnika ineba seatjolaha. Indakgipa obostarango 
bilgriana bate bilakdugaan nambata jedakode chang-sanon pilakkon 
matchotatna man gen. Capt. Williamsonni antidamrangko chipna 
janape on anirangkoba namnikpaa ineba u-iataha. Aroba, Capt. 
Williamsonni nangnikgipa European Officerrangko on-na man-gen 
aro India sorkari hukum on-on uamangko watatgen ineba 
agandapataha. 16

man-soe, India sorkarini Secretary (Foreign Deptt), C.U. Aitchison 
indake Bengal Sorkarini Secretaryna 1 872 ni September 3 tariko 
sechakaha aro hukumko on-ataha-British sorkarini nokgipa ong-aniko 
A chik a-songna mesokna aro bangki jakgitelgipa A chik songrangko 
bamatna tarisamsoanirangko aro uamangko Garo Hillsni D.C. ni 
ning-ona ra-bana am-aniko aro apsan somoeon chadrapgipa 
songrangko sasti on-na aro mande so-otgiparangko sorkarina rim-e 
on-na janapanirangko namnikaha.

songrango napon bobil dingna nangja indiba uamangko bamatnasa 
kam ka-na nanga aro Laskerrang aro Nokmarang sorkarina 
dakchakaniko on-na nanga. Sipai dol bang-dugana nangja indiba 
chu-onga gita silchidarerang dongna nanga. Lieutenant-Governor

a-baona re-bapilahajeon uamang waltuaha. Manda songni Nokma,

Wajan sorkarina bamaniko dakaha. Ia songgitalo nok 21, measa 
sak 35, me-chik sak 30 aro bi sarang sak 18 dongachim. 1 p.m.o 
jakgitelgipa Gabil songni Nokma, Songman re-bae kajinako game 
bameaha. Gabil songara uanoni Salgipengchipak salsani rama 
ong achim. Jakgitelgipa Grab minggipa songni Nokmaba re-bae 
bameaha. Nokmako a-tipo tuate pringgipino uni songchi re-na tik 
ka-aha. Manda, Gabil aro Grabni Nokmarang Bolonggiri, Sinai aro 
Jogagiri scxigrangna dakaniko knae aro nike uandaka obostao ga-akna 
kene uamang sorkarina bametokaha. December 10 tariko Dukigiri 
songo walo Capt. Davies aro uni dolko ro-ong gogrimanio 
Bolonggiri, Sinai, Grab aro Mandani manderangba jak on-paachim 
ine Capt Davies ma-siaha. Gabilni Nokmani aganani gita uni songo 
nok 19, me asa sak 25, me-chik sak 33 aro sak 20 bi-sa-desarang 
dongachim.

SASONNI BEWAL ARO NIAMRANG, 1873 - 1912 

1867 oni 1872 ona bilsi bongana Garo Hillsko dokkol kao 
British sorkari Turao skotong songjinmako rikna a-bachengaha, 
Nokmarangko ong-nike ra-chake, skango David Scottni daka gita 
uamangoninkon Laskerrang seokaha, aro uamangkon kajinarangko 
gilate sorkariko dakchakataha, Thanarangko kulie Policerangko 
donaha, Daluoni Turaona aro Turaoni Mankacharona mile 100 mang 
namgipa ramako cho-aha, chadrapanirangko aro skona sikar 
ka-anirangko bon-ataha, chasongni chasongna anchini pal an-china 
a jak sokna chinganiko gimaataha, skatang niam gri janggi 
tangaoniko tom- tome janggitangna ka sne ra baaha, American Baptist

Missionaryrangko lekka porako a bachengna on aha aro Garo Hills 
gimikkon dokkol ka-srange survey ka e, maprangko sale British 
Sorkarini sasonona ra-batokaha. 1 

Policeko Donani : 

Cooch Behar Divisionni Commissionerni chanchiani gita 
kenchakani gribatsrange aro chusokbatsrange Achikrangko sason 
ka nade Maharirangni skotong ba Nokmarangni jakchiba ba je 
mandekon Maharirang ra chaka, uamangchisa man gen. Policerangni 
jakchi sason ka-atode uamangko batesa ka onangatgen ineba ua 
chanchiachim. Garo Hillsni Policerangkode Military Guard ba Sipai 
nitimgiparang gitasa ra-na nanggen ineba ua chanchiachim. 
Chong-motko agangenchim ongode, banggija somocrangna skang, 
Garo Hillsni D.C., British sorkarina kakket ong gipa 
Nokmarangoniko Laskerrang songe, kamo rakkiani lekkarangko 
onahachim. 

Man a dipet songrango Police Thana kulianiko gelchina 
Commissioner Capt. Williamsonko ku pattiaha maina uarang 
officerrangko duk on a aro jajrengatachim aro songo noko 
donggiparangkoba uamangna dak ra-rura na aro ration olm nangani 
gimin ka-o nangatachim. Thanako minggni miksonganiosan kulina

Kosako janapgipako sorkari namnikaha indiba Policerangko

da-o dongana bate bariatna jechakaha. 3 

Laskerni Sasonko Ra'gata : 

Achikrangko sason ka-gipa aro nirokgipa A-king Nokmana 
agre darang gipin grichim. Laskerchi sason aro bichal ka-ani bewalko 
British sorkarisa Garo Hillso ra-gatchengaha. “Lasker” katta Persian 
ku sikoni ong-a jeni orto elakani skotong ong-achim. Muslimrang 
Indiaona re-bao Laskerni sasonko ra baaha aro uako Britishrang Garo 
Hillso ragataha. 4 Garo Hills Autonomous District Councilba ukon 
rachakaha. 5 

Sason aro bichalko Laskerni jakchi dakatna ine David Scott 
1819 bilsio janape on-chengaha. Policeni aro bichalni kamrangko 
Laskema on-na gita Scott janaptaiaha. Laskerni bichalna man-gijako 
Panchaetchi bichalatna nanga ineba janape on-aha. 6 Ia pilak janape 
on-anirangko British sorkari namnike 1822 ni September 19 tariko 
ra gataha. Indakesa Laskerni system Garo Hillso atchiaha. 7 

Je Nokmarangan British sorkariko ra-chakachim aro 
elakatangtangko bilsi gimiko tom-tome rakkiachim, uamangna 
Allowance aro gipin boksisrangko (chu bottol aro ba-rarang) on-na 
gita sanction ka-china David Scott sorkarina 1824 ni December 30 
tariko see watataha. Sorkariba uko dakna namnike ra chakaha. Uni 
jamano, David Scott ka-mao mesokgipa elakarangni laskerrang baksa 
songgigrikaniko dake uamangna Allowance aro boksisrangko 
on-chengaha. Uandake iamangan 1824 bilsio Garo Hillsni 
elakarangni Laskerrang ong-chengachim.

Bengal, No. 652 P., dt. Fort William, the 3rd, April, 1 873.

Lasker Songani Bewal : 

David Scottni Lasker songchengo ua Nokmarangni gisepo 
u ibatgipa aro katta maniatbatgipako Lasker songachim. Aroba 
uamangoniko, kajinarangko gile on ronggen aro sorkarina kakket 
ong-gen ine kurachakatchengesa songachim. 12 1866 aro 1873 
bilsirango Capt. Williamson Laskerrangko songoba Nokmarangni

gisepo seng-batgipa aro sason changbatgiparangkosa Lasker 
songachim. 13 

Britishni chasongode, jolprakprakni Nokmarangko D.C. 
okamchimonge uamangoni saksakon Lasker ongna mingchina 
gcetrongachim. MinggiminkosaD.C. Commissionernarachakchina 
watatrongachim. 14 

Garo Hills Autonomous District Councilni ningo, District 
Councilni Executive Committee Lasker songna bil man-aha. 
A bachengode elakatangtango electionko ong atachim indiba 
ja-mano Executive Committee mandetangtangko jekoba Lasker 
songaiaha. Indake Lasker songara Political ong baaha. 1964 bilsio 
Garo Hills District Councilni tik ka gimin gita, Lasker ong na ia 
gunrang dongna nanga : 

Ua bilsi 35 na komina nangja; uo namgipacholon aro kakketani 
dongna nanga; jatni niam-ritirangko u igipa ong-na nanga; tangka 
paisa donggipa aro bilsio Nokni kajinako gile mannagipaoniko bak 
brini baksako onsochengna man gipa ong na nanga. 15

Policeni kamrang : Mande so-oton ba maiba dos dakon 
Laskerrang Policeni kamko kana nanggen. Elakatangtango pilak 
dos dakgiparangko Laskerrang rim-na nanggen aro elakatangtangko 
tom-tome rakkina nanggen. 

2. Elakatangtango pilak dos dakgiparangko, sibewal 
sigijagiparangko aro maiba kolona ong-giparangko Policena ba 
D.C. na nalis ka na nanga. 

3. Elakatango cha-ua, dokpreta, noko napdrae raseka, 
tilekdraarang dongode Lasker bakbakan ua biapona re-ange 
sandiena nanga. Ua bichalna man-jaode D.C. na sal 8 ni gisepo 
nalis ka na nanga. 

4. Dos dake katgiparangko elakatangoniko rike rim na nanga. 
Gipin elakachi katnapahaode uni Laskerna u-iatna nanga. 

5. Dos dakgipako rim-na man-jaode D.C. na ba Policena on-na 
nanga. 

6. Chu peke golmal ka-giparangko aro gipin golmal dakgiparangko 
aro joa kalgiparangko Lasker rim-na nanga aro dai gamatna 
nanga. 

7. Je songan dos dakgiparangko rim e on-na man-ja, Lasker 
uamangko dai gamatna nanga.

8. Laskemi bichalko D.C. na apil ka na man-gen. 

Criminalni Bilrang : 

Elakatangtango Laskerrang ka-mao mesokgipa dos 
dakgiparangko bichal ka-gen : 

1. Cha-uani, mal-mata ra seka, dokpreta, noko napdraa aro 
maneng-saia. 

2. Laskerrang Rs. 50 ona dai ratna man-gen. 

3. Bichal ratani bakan, apil ka-jaode, bichalko rate on-gen aro 
gamna nangako gamatgen. 

4. Iamangko Lasker bichal ka-na man-jawa : 

Pagipa, ma-gipa, degipa, jikgipa ba dedrang aro ma-drang. 
Assami A-chik ong-jaode, Garo Hills Districtni A-chik ong-jaode 
ba dos dakgipa sorkarini kosako dos dakode, iarangko bichalna 
man-jawa. 

5. Lasker a-pal biapo sak 3 sakkirangchi bichal ka-gen. 

6. Laskemi bichalko see rakkirikani dongjawa. 

7. Dai gamatgipa tangkako D.C. na ba uni pal ge-etgimin officema 
on-gen. 



[203]

Civilni Bilrang : 

1 . Lasker iamangko bichal ka-na manjawa : 

Pagipa, ma-gipa, jikgipa, dedrang, mama-ada, no-namchik aro 
ma-drang, nalis ka-gipa aro Assami elakatangni Achik ong-na 
nanggen. Pilak bichalko a-palo sakki sak 3 chi bichal ka-gen. 

2. Dos dakgiparangko bichalona re-baatna bil donggen aro Rs. 
50/- ona dai ratgen. 

3. Pilak bichalko ku-sik agananichi, serikani gri bichalgen. 
Dolgniko sing-grik-sangrike niam-ritio pangchake bichale 
on-gen. 

4. Dos dakgiparangko Lasker hajoto sal 8 ba rakkina mangen. 

5. Pilak u-iatanirangko Lasker ku-sikchi u-iataigen. 

6. Jerangko Laskerrang bichalna man-ja uarangko D.C. na 
pakwatgen. 

7. Ua caseko Lasker man-jae dolgnian A chik ong-ode, 
Panchaetona D.C. watate bichal kaatgen. 

8. Laskerrrangni pilak bichalrangkon D.C. na apil ka-na man gen. 16 
la niamrangkon da-alona kingking Laskerni bichalo 

jakkalkuenga.

nanggen; aro ua mauza ning-o nokdang baksa dongna nanggen.

ong-gen. Mandal aro Kanungorang badiaba a-palko ong-gija kajina 
gatode uan uko sorkarina de-e mesokna nanggen. Uni gipin kamrang-

D.C. ni ge-etako man-jaskal Chong motgipa Policerang Policeni 
kamko ka-jawa. Uarangko Nokmarang aro Laskerrang ka-gen. 
Uamang pilak dosi dakgiparangko rim-gen aro elakatangtango 
tom-tomaniko ra-bagen. Uamang pilak dosi dakgiparangko sibewal 
sigijagiparangko aro pilak Kotona ong-giparangko D.C. na nalis 
ka gen. Nokma aro Laskerrang dos dakgiparangko rim-na man jaode 
Chong-motgipa Policerangni dakchakaniko D.C.o bi-gen. 
A-kingtangtango ba elakatangtango re-jojoe roaigipa ba namgijagipa 
aro ka-dongna man-gijagipa manderangko Nokmarang aro 
Laskerrang nirokgen aro D.C. na nalis ka-gen. 

Tom-tomaniko man na aro dosi dakgiparangko rim-na a-songo 
pilak songdonggiparangan Policerangko aro Lasker aro 
Nokmarangko dakchakna nanga. Dakchakaniko on-jaode 
songgimikkon Laskerchi ba D.C. chi dai gamatgen. Badia 
manderang dakchakaniko on-na manjaha ukoba u-ijaode, uni 
Laskerrang aro Nokmarang aganchakskana nanggen. Dosi 
dakgiparangko u-ina ba rim-na man-gijaniara songni manderangni 
gimin ong-a ine nikode, D.C. songgimikkon dai gamatgen.

Civil Rules : Garo Hillso Civil Justiceko D.C., Assistant 
Commissioner aro Laskerrangni jakchi kam ka-gen. Elakatangni 
chonchongipa mamlarangko Lasker bichal ka-gen indiba ma-drang 
bakskarangko bichal ka-na man-jawa. Laskerrang jinmani mikkango 
sakki sak 3 chi bichal ka-gen aro Rs. 50/- ona dai ratna man-gen. 
Laskemi bichalni kosako apil ka-a dongjaode ua rang-sanan dai 
ratgiminko chu-sokatgen aro dosi dakgipa manderangko sal 8 na ba 
raiko chu-sokatkujana kingking rakkigen. Laskemi bichalni kosako 
D.C. naba Assistant Commissionema apil ka-na man -gen. Laskemi 
bichalo tik ong-ja ine nikode, D.C. ba Asstt. Commissioner 
a-bachenge bichal ka-gen ba Panchaetni jakchi bichal ka-atgen. 

Mamla ka-giparang A-chikrang ong-genchimode D.C. 
uamangko Panchaetni jakchi bichal ka-atgen. Bichal ka-giparangko, 
dol gni mamla ka-giparang apsan nomboro minggen ba bichal ka-na 
minga man-gipa manderang saksa bichal ka-gipa Pamongko 
mingskagen. Bichal ka-na minga man-giparang aro uamangni 
minggipa Pamongni bimingrang aro dongramrangko see rakkigen. 
Indakgipa Panchaetko tom-atna aro sakkirangko Laskerrang sal 8 
ni gisepo okamgen. Indakgipa bichalni kosako apil ka-ani dongjawa. 



[209] 


Raio ama man-giparangni nokrangko, ganding-chinding, 
me-dik-samdik aro atte-gitchirangko korok ka-jawa ba paljawa ba 
on-atjawa jena kingking mamla iarangna ong-jachim. Indiba uni 
a-arangko palna ba on-atskana mangen. 

Gro nanganirangna sal 8 na bate Patoko donatna man-jawa

man gen. Dos dakgipa manderang Garo Hills Districtni a-paloni 
ong-ode uko bichal ka-na man-jawa indiba uni mamlako ua

Districtonan watatskagen.

dongjawa indiba Commissioner nangnikode rai nokolko bi-ate 
nitaipilna man gen aro dingtang raikoba on-na man gen.

Procedureni miksongani gita bichal ka-gen. Ukil ba Muktearko 
baltiatna man-jawa.

dol gnian nangnikode aro sakki on-giparangkoba ku-mikchetatna 
nangnikode, Kachari mande saksako done uni mikkango 
an-tangtangko gimaaona galone ku-mikchetatgen. Christianrang

Mohammedanrang rongjrigipakattarangchi ku-mikchetgen.

mal-bosturangko korok ka-e gamatpilna Laskerrang bil man-gen.

INNER LINE REGULATIONS 

A-kaweni Banggal-rorirangni a-brini A-chikrang baksa skatang 
bading-chiwalgrikaniara basakoba basakoba dal-a golmal- 
gondokolrangko ong-atrongachim. Iarang mongsongbate bolrang, 
sterang, mongma wagamrang, kil aro gipin gipin buringni 
bosturangko bading-chiwalgrikanio ong-rongbatachim, jerangko 
brena uamang bilongen susagriktokachim. Indake ong-taitaianiko 
champengna gita a-kaweo songdonggipa Britishni retrangko Pass 
ba Licence gri badiaba salgimin ari-simani nalsachi re-angna beng-na 
nanga ine British sorkari nangnikahachim. 37 Uni gimin Inner Line 
Regulations jekon “Bengal Eastern Frontier Regulation I of 1873” 
ine mingachim uko Kamrup, Darrang, Nagaon, Sibsagar, Lakhimpur, 
Garo Hills, Khasi aro Jaintia Hills, Naga Hills, Cachar aro Chittagong 
Hills Districtrango tom-tomanina aro nama sasonona ra bana gita 
1873 ni November 1 tariko ra-gataha.

2. Kosako janapbagipa District antini simani ning-o Inner Line 
ba ning-ani ariko saltaigen jeni nalsachi je rokom Britishni retrangko 
Pass ba Licence gri re-angna on-jawachim, je Passkon D.C. 
on-genchim. 

35. Govt, of Bengal. File No. 70 (Judicial) of 1 874, The Scheduled Districts Act 
XIV of 1874. 

36. The Assam Gazette Notifications of 1910 and 1937. 

37. E. Gait, A History of Assam, 1969, PP. 386 - 87. 



[ 211 ] 


3. A-chik ong-gija saoba gipin mande ia Inner Lineko Pass gri 
ga-bate re-angode ua skanggipa chango Rs. 100/- ko dai gamna 
nanggen aro ja-mano manitaijaode Rs. 500/- nabatgija bajagittamna 
patok dongna nanggen aro maniku-jaode daikoba gamna aro 
patokoba dongna nanggen. 

4. Ia Pass ba Licenceni form ba lekkako D.C. tarigen aro Pass 
ni damkoba uan tik kagen. 

5. Je mandean ia niamko pe-a aro uoniko rangket, ste, mongma 
wagam aro gipin buringni bosturangko man-a, ua bosturangko sorkari 
korok kagen. 

6. Inner Lineko ga bate re-angna beng-gipa mandeko Inner 
Lineni nalsachi nikode aro Inner Line Passko ua mesokna jechakode 
ba mesokna man-jaode ba Pass dongeba Inner Lineni gipin 
niamrangko pe-ode sorkarini bil on gimin je mandeba uko rimna 
man-gen.

7. Je British sorkarini ret ba gipinba janapbagipa Districto 
atchigipa aro songdongkamgipa ong-jaode Districtni Inner Line 
salgipani nalsachi a-ako ba a-ani mal-gamko sorkarini hukum gri 
ra-na man-jawa. 

8. Je mandean officemi see on-ani hukum gri mongmako so-ota 
ba rim-a ba so-otna ba rim na jotton ka-a ba indakgiparangko 
dakchaka, indakgiparangko mongma mangprakna Rs. 200/- ko 
gamatgen aro mongma so-otgimin ba rim-giminko sorkari ra-pilgen. 
Indiba an-tangko ba Noktangko ba a-bao ge-a gamarangko 
chelchakna dakode ukode sasti on-jawa indiba mongmakode 
sorkarina on-na nanggen. 

9. Mongmako so-otna ba rim na Licenceko on-na niamrangko 
District officerrang dakgen. 

10. Dos dakani jadokni gisepon dos dakgiparangko bichal ka-e 
rai on-gen. 

11. Pilak dos dakgiparangkon Districtni 1st ba 2nd class 
Magistraterang bichal ka-gen aro jamin ra-na man-gen. 38

1873.

Indiba Garo Hills gipin Districtrangoni dingtangani gimin ia 
Inner Line Regulationsko name chu-sokatna man-jawa ine Assamni

Chief Commissioner India sorkarini Secretaryna No. 178, dated 
Shillong, the 3rd June, 1 874 tariko see u-iataha. Ua neng-nikarangko 
indake seataha : 

1 . Garo Hillsde B ritish sorkarini a-dok aro A-chik manderangko 
gipin sorkarini retrangoni chelchakna nanggen, indiba Naga Hills 
aro gipinrango aditana British sorkarini ning ogipa manderangko 
gipin Districtrangni manderangoniko chelchakna nanggen. Uni gimin 
Inner Line Regulationko Garo Hillso rake chu sokatna man-jawa. 
Aroba a-kawe aro a-brini manderangni gisepo kilrangko aro gipin 
bosturangko bading-chiwalgrikani namen tom-tome ong-engani 
gimin Inner Lineko saltaiode uarang bon angskagen. 

2. Garo Hillsni simarangko pilakchikon saltokman-aha. Uni 
gimin Districtni simarangko a-ao cho-e salkingjaoba, sima salna tik 
ka-gimin gitan Inner Line dake ra-e bading-chiwalgiparangna Pass 
on-skachina Chief Commissioner janape on-aha. Garo Hillsni Salgro, 
Saliram aro Salgipengchi Districtni simarang dake salgiminkon Inner 
Line dake ra-on namaigen ineba ua chanchiaha. Salaramchipakode 
Inner Line dakatna nangnikjachim maina Khasi aro A-chikrangni 
gisepo golmal ong-gniko niksamsojachim. A-kawerango aro 
a-brirango songdonggiparangni giseposa golmalrang 
ong-rongbatachim. 

3. Garo Hillsni a-brirangchi an-pagipa a-kawerangko aditan 
Jomindarrang dabiengani gimin uamangni dabiengako knachakna 
skangan Inner Lineko tik ka-chengna nanga ineba His Excellency, 
the Viceroy-in-Council chanchiaha. 

4. Aroba Garo Hillso koilarang bang-en donga ine nikani gimin 
Jomindarrang uarangnaba dabianiko dakna skangan Inner Lineko 
ta-raken dakna nanggen. 

5. Inner Lineni niamrangko Garo Hillsoba ra-gate kam ka-gen 
ine tik ka-manaha aro Chief Commissioner iakode jegaljachim indiba

bilsi gnina skangmangmangsan jakgitel ong-aniko sorkarini sasonona 
re-bae uamangko sason ka-na niamrangko aro cholrangko 
dakengku-ani gimin uarangko matchotchengon namgen ine Chief 
Commissioner skachim. Uni ja-manosa Inner Lineko ra-gate kam 
ka-o nambatgen ineba ua chanchiaha. 



[213] 


6. Uni gimin Garo Hillso Inner Line Regulationni pilakkode 
kam ka-jawachim. Garo Hillsona re-anggipa pilak a-kaweni 
inanderangde Pass rana nangjawa maina uamangni gisepo bading- 
chiwalgrikani namgni ong-achim. Uni gimin, mongma rim-giparang 
aro uamangko dakchakgiparang, bol den-giparang, sikar ka-giparang, 
slai aro gulirangko badinggiparang, rangketko chimonggiparang, 
mongma wagam chimonggiparang aro Tika su-giparangsaan Pass 
rana nanggenchim. 

7. Pass ra-na nanggipa manderangko obostani kri ja-mano 
hariatgen aro D.C. an Passko on-gen. Pass kingprakna anna briko 
gamna nanggen. 39 

Viceroy aro Governor-General-in-Councilni chanchiani gita 
Chief Commissioner Inner Line Regulationsni miksongani aro kamni 
a-bako ma-sisretaha. Inner Line Regulationni miksonganide dam 
rakbatao pale lapko man-na ka-donge bosturangko bree 
chimonggiparangni A-chik a-dokrangona bingbang re-ange rope 
chope a-arangko bree ba uamangni buringni bosturangko tol-e bike 
ra-bae ia mikmalgijagipa aro skia man-gijagiparang baksa ja-gue 
ra-aoniko champengna gitasa ong-achim. 40

Commissioner sechakaha. Ia obostarang Garo Hillsoba ong-enga.

SORKARINI A BACHENGGIPA KAMRANG, 1873-1874 

Captain W. J. Williamson 1873 bilsio Garo Hills Districtni D.C. 
ongachim aro 1873 ni April jaoni Octoberona Mr. Reily Garo Hills 
Districtni Police Superintendent ong-e gipin Districtrangoni re bae 
Garo Hillso chu-onga gita Police rakkina mankujana kingking kam 
ka giparangniba skotong ong aha. Mr. Reily ua bilsio Assistant 
Commissionerba ongaha. 1 

Mr. Reily D. C. na bang a dakchakanirangko on aha aro uni 
dongjarango, uni pal kam kasoaha. Ua Turao chonchongipa 
dokanrangko kuliate, bading-chiwalaniko a bachengate officeni 
manderangna pilak nanganirangko palanio D.C. ko 
dakchakbeahachim. Uni Mymensing District aro uni manderangkoba 
namen uiani, a-kawe aro Turani gisepo bading-chiwalgrikaniko 
dakatna aro dalatna dakchakbeani ongachim. 

Mr. Cawley, gital manderangko Policena rakkianio aro 
uamangko skianio namgipa kamko dakangahachim. Ua A-chik 
a dokrangko aro manderangko name masiani gimin ua 
dakchakanirang D.C. na namen gamchatgiparang ong achim. 

1873 ni April jao D.C. Khasi aro Garo Hillsni sima-arirangko 
songani ja-mano Turaona re-bae October jaona kingking Turaon 
dongbatahachim, gisep giseposan ua Mankachar aro Rohumario kam 
kaarangko nirokna reangaiachim. 

1873 ni August jao Mr. Cawleyko Banggirini Nokmako rim na

watataha, jean Policeko aro Laskerkoba manijachim. Ia Nokmara 
songtangni mande saksa baksa montol dakani bidingo kajia 
ongachim. Mr. Cawley dol gnikon rim baaha aro D.C. bichal kae, 
Nokmako katta manijana sasti one namatc donaha. Bugi chibimani 
rikamo anti kulina gita nina D.C. October jao Bugini sambaogipa 
Selbal songona re angaha. Skango ua biapo anti dongachim indiba 
jakgitelgipa Achikrang Bengali badinggipa saksako sootani gimin 
ua anti bon ahachim. David Scottba ua biapon guare rike sipairangko

1874 bilsio Garo Hillsni D.C. ni report on-ani gita A-chikrango 
bosturangko jakkalna dakani karkanarang brae dongjachim. Uamang 
an-tangtang ganna gita re-king aro gando makkalrangkode dakna 
changachim. Ua ba-rarangko dokani kolde a-kaweo gitan apsanchim. 
Uamang Am-pakkoba dokna changachim. Ba-rarangko nil boloniko 
rongba sim-rongachim. Sissu Dagai aro Chengon bolrangonikoba 
gitchak rongchi rong sim-rongachim. Garo Hillsni Salaramchipako 
man-gipa maibani bikrokrangko ro-ongo ba rong-brakrango ka-snc 
ka-sne natesa uamang ripokrangko dakachim. 

Mitam songrango A-chikrang mc-dik, sam-dik aro 
basingrangkoba dakna changachim indiba rinokede dakna 
man-jachim indiba kamde choliaiachim. Uamangna ringchak. 
cha-chak aro song-chakna gita Nokgipaan Launi bekrangko, 
e-salrangko aro wa-arangko on-ahachim. Indake altue jakkalna 
bosturangko man-aiani gimin uamangko maiba gitalko dakna 
draatani dongjachim. Uamangni neng-ani bang-ja aro uarangko altuen 
chu-sokatna man-aiachim. Mistri kam ka-e cha-gipa dongjachim

indiba uamang nok rikna aro wa-achi watani kamrangko, jekae, Sepi, 
am, kokcheng, kera, ruan, gitchera, chek aro indakgiparangko watna 
chengnachangtokachim. Mitam songrango atte, gitchi, mil-am aro 
sel-urangko dokna gitchambegipa mulrangko jakkalachim. Indiba 
ja-mano Mymensingni inanderang uarangko dake palani gimin 
A-chikrang uarangko dakangskajahachim.

SASONNI BIDINGO KAMRANG

A-chikrangni bon-kamgipa jakgitelaniko 1872-73 bilsirango 
nikgrikaha. 1873 bilsio bon-kamgipa A-chik song British sorkarina 
bamaha. Indioba A-chikrangni bamaniko British sorkari bebe ra-na 
man-kujachim aro Policerangko ra-angpilode uamang golma) 
ka-taigen ine chanchiachim. Garo Hillsni D.C. de ia bamani 
bamchongmotani ong-a aro A-chikrang da-o sorkarini ning-o tomtom 
dongna skani gisik dongchongmotaha ine chanchiskachim. 
Uandaken re-anggipabilsisao dal-a a-sel ong-a dongjahachim. Chona 
mingsa ba minggnisan a-sel ong-a dongaiachim. Ia chonchona 
a-selrangdongengkuani gimin Lieut-Govemor, Sir George Campbell, 
Rongrenggirio Police Stationko dongangkuna nanga ine inaha. 
Beanbebe A-chikrangni dakrongbagipa, chong-motan, dal-gipa 
mande sio mande saksa ba sakgniko boli on e uko A-chikrangni 
Salgi Chitmangona dilangna nanga ingipa bebera-ani gimakujachim. 
Uni gimin iandakgipa bebera-ani gimakujana kingking mande skona 
sikar ka-ani bewal bon-kujawachim. Indiba iarang raka sasono 
ta-raken gimaangengaha ine uamang nikaha. 

D.C. ni report gita, 1873 - 74 bilsirango dal-a sko sotgrikani 
a-sel ong-a dongjachim jean skango ramramsachim. Bang-bata a-sel 
ong-arangan chonchonachim aro uarangko Laskerrangan Panchaeto 
bichal ka-e bon-atna man-achim. Uamangoni saksa Simsanggreni 
Thamreng Lasker ong-achim jean uni elakao mande so-otgipako 
rim e on-ahachim. Indiba pilak Laskerrangande kakket ong-e kam 
ka-a ong-jachim. A-beng jolni Mailing Lasker elakatango mande 
so-ote ba ja-gao nange siako sorkarina nalis ka-jani gimin dai 
gamatako man- aha. Indiba ka-sne ka-sne Laskerrang manderangni 
kosako bil man-roroangtokengaha aro 1874 bilsio wachi karina 
Rongrengo Policerangna merong aro gipin nanganirangko ripingsona 
merong bosta 350 rangko Tura aro Durgapuroniko rettangtangko 
oldoatna man-aha. Chakchikachi aro name nirokanichisa Lasker aro 
Nokmarangni sasonko mangrakatna man-gen ine D.C. nikaha. Uni 
gimin Laskemi jakko bilakatna gita Laskerrangni elakarangko tik 
ka-chengna nanggen ineba nikaha. Garo Hillso Lasker Systemko

[229] 


David Scott 1822 bilsio a-bachengatahachim aro uko Capt. Morton 
1865 bilsio ra-gattaiaha. Ia somoerango Garo Hillsni Mapko 
salkujachim. Uni gimin uamangni skanggipa Laskerrangko 
rakkichengo Nokmarangni aro manderangni gisepo bil 
inan-batgiparangko Lasker songe uamang badia songrangko sason 
man-gen uamango sing e uako uamangni Elaka dake sale 
on-aiachim. Uni giminsa Damra jolchi mitam Laskemi Elakarangde 
salgni re-na nanggipa chel aosa dongachim aro Laskemi song aro

damprako song 10 oni 15 ba una bate songrangko donna nangnike 
ja-mano donahachim. 

GRO DINGANI, SKO SOTANI 


1873 - 74 bilsini report gita, bang-a mahari maharini gisepo 
an-china gro dinganiko D.C. bichal kae namatna man-ahachim. 
1872 - 73 bilsio jakgitelgipa A-chik songrangko dokkol ka-na skang, 
chasongni chasongna a-ana bobil dinggrikani, sko sotgrikani aro 
anchi onggrikanirang banga songrangon dongkuengachim. Indake 
gro dinggrikanio dolgnionin banga janggirang gimabeahachim. Uni 
gimin D.C. gro dinggipa pilak maharirangkon okamate, bichal ka e, 
uamangni donkuenggipa sko bikrokrangko ra-baate dol gnini 
mikkangon see galattokachim aro chu ongrike ba kangrikate bobil 
dinggrikaniko namatahachim. Indiomangba 1874 bilsio skango bobil 
dinggrike sko sotgrikani gro mingchet dongkuengachim. Mingsa 
groara Shemsonggiri songo mande so-otani ongachim. Shemsonggiri 
songni mande saksa aro Dawa songni saksa chu ringmitingo

ringrimgipa saksako so otahachim, D.C. bichal ka e saksako bilsi 6 
na aro sakgipinko bilsi 7 na patok donataha. 

Minggipin groara, segipani jikgipako so-otani ong achim. 
Segipa chu peke jikgipako kajia botaha. Jikgipani do-ga chipe katon 
ua jikgipako sel-uchi su-pretate siataha. Mikselon ua duk ong-bee 
an-tangkoba siatna am ahachim indiba man-changjaha. Uko bichal 
ka e bilsi 5 na patok donataha. 



[230] 


Gabil songo mande sakbri saksa mandeko den aha aro sakbrikon 
D.C. de Andamanchi watatna rai on-ahachim indiba Bengalni Lt. 
Governor sastiko komiate bilsibrina patok donatskaaha. 

Minggipa aselara, nok nokgipa chakkol me-asa bi sako alu 
cha-ua aro kam aratani gimin rim e aao gostapahachim aro a-ao 
gaake dongmitingo boltongchi dokon ua bi-sa siahachim. Ua nokgipa 
siede dokna mangsonga ong-ja ine nike uko bilsisnina patok 
donataha. 

Mingsa a selara Songsak songo ong-achim. Songsakni mande 
saksa buringchi roangengachim. Unon buringgrimoni mande sakgni 
rangsan bilprubaaha. Uko mapil ine chanchie seluchi su-aton saksa 
siaha. Ian chong mot ong a ine nike sasti on-jaha. 

Bongiri songni Raicheng minggipako apsan songni Jongsing 
minggipa montol dakgipa ine potahani gimin uamangni gisepo dal a 
jegrikani ong-aha. Uamangko Lasker bichal ka-na am on Jongsing 
rebanajechakaha. Police sakgniko watate Jongsingko rimnaamode 
songni manderang warachake rim bana man jaha. Uni gimin D.C. 
an-tangan songona re-bae Raichang aro Jongsingni jegrikaniko

namate donangaha. Untal ua songba tom-tome dongangaha. 

Ruuta bilsirangna skang bobil dinggrikani ming 3 gro dakgipako 
bichal kae namate donaha. 1874 bilsio cha-uani case ming 15 
dongachim indiba chonchonasachim. Uarangoni mingsara, sorkario 
kam ka-gipa saksa Ram Doyal minggipa tangka Rs. 14/- ko Post 
Bag oniko cha-uahachim. Ua dostangko ku-rachakahani gimin uko 
Rs. 20/- dai gamate bilsignina patoko donatahachim. 

1874 bilsio mingsa dal-a mokordoma a-ani bidingo Rongbuni 
Gilgep Lasker aro Goalparani Collectomi gisepo nakataha indiba 
Goalpara aro Garo Hillsni ari-simarangko a ao salkujani gimin ia 
caseko dontongataha. Iarangna agreba bang-a grorangkon Panchaeto 
aro Laskerrang bichal ka-e bon-atahachim. 

Ia bilsio sibewal sigija sak 14 siahachim. Uamangoni sak 7 ko 
matcha ba mapil chikachim; sak 4 borangoni ga ake siachim; sak 
gni kae siachim aro saksa chio sripe siachim. Iarangna agreba, diltua 
biaprangoba rokom rokom bewalrango sigiparangba 
dongengkuenggenchim. 



[231] 

GARO HILLSNI POLICE 


1873 ni March 1 tariko, Garo Hillso Policeko sak 150 oni sak 
302 ona bariataha aro officerrangkoba bang-batataha. Garo Hills 
gimikko dokkol ka-ani gimin nirokna nangani a-dokrang bariaha. 
Uni gimin Rongrengo sak 50 Policerangko donaha aro Damalgiri 
aro Bugi rikamo donggipa Selbal songoba Policerangko donaha. 

Banga a-selrangni gimin Policeo napatna manderangko manna

neng-achim. Uamangoni milamrangara, biapni dongansengjani, gipin 
gipin kamrangko ka-atani aro Assam Regiment aro Cachar Policeosa 
namnitbatachim. 

Garo Hillsni Policeo Nepalrang bang-batachim. Gipinrangara 
A-chikrang, Rabharang, Kacharirang aro Kochrang ongachim. Ia 
jat brikon Jharua ineba mingachim. Mr. Cawley, ia gital sak 200 
Policeo napgiparangna trainingko on-ahachim aro Mr. Metcalfe 
re-bae niange kusi ong-beaha. 

Achikrangni chu song-ako nikon banga Policerangan ringna 
skakon chakna man-jachim. Uandaken uamangoni chu 
peksimsimaigiparang gnangchim. Nepali Policerang joa 
kal-rakgiparangba ong achim. Uani giminan uamang sasti 
man-rongachim. Police Linekoba uamangna pame rike on-ahachim. 
Lineprakni gisepgriko apalgipa rama, chi jokangani aro chiringni 
chikoba salbae on-achim jedakode wa-al kamoba chiko jakkalna 
mangenchim. 

Slai goaniko ranta ka-na gital biapo, chong-motan, da-oni 
Chandmario 1875 bilsio dakna tik ka-e donaha. Mr. Livesay Police 
officer ong-e 1873 ni February jao Turaona re-baaha aro Ram Charan 
Inspector ong-aha. 

Post Office : 1873 bilsio Turao Post Office dongkujachim. 
Dhubrioni Turaona dak ra-timgipasan chittirangko Turaona ra-bae 
sualaiachim. Turaoni Singimariona dak ra-timgipa sorkari mande 
ong-achim indiba Singimarioni Dhubriona dak ra-timgipa Jomindanu 
mande ong-achim jekon sorkari ra-china gita Cooch Behami 
Commissioner hukum on-ahachim. Turaoni Shillongonaba dak 
ra-rurana sorkari chanchiaha. 



[232]

Sorokrang : 1873-74 bilsio Garo Hills Districtna sorokrangko 
cho-na Bengal sorkari tangka Rs. 50,000/- ko onaha. Badia 
sorokrangko pugen aro korosrang badita nanggen uarangko tik 
ka-manahachim. Aroba aditakode chomangnokchim indiba 
Mankacharo merongni dam rang san rakdoahani giminsa 
Daijeelingchiniko kulirangko bang-e rimbana chol ongjaha. A-chik 
adokrango kuli man-brejachim, uni gimin je man-achi, Mankacharoni 
Harigaonona matchu gari salna mane sorok dilsako chochengaha 
maina ia Garo Hillsko Songdu chibimaona sokatachim. Ia sorok 
cho ako nitimgipa Overseer Babu Ram Lai ong achim aro uni gisik 
nange cho*dilanichi Rohumarioni Harigaonona gimik mile 20 mang 
ong-e motor gari rena man-pile cho-ahachim. Gitcham sorokrangko 
namdapataha aro Ayodoba jolo gonggegipa sorokko srongate adha 
mile mangko komiatahachim. Re-baenggipa bilsio iako algatra one 
tarina janapahachim maina ro-ongrangko ramani samtangtangchin 
altuaen man-aigenchim. Chibimarangko wa ani dolongrangchi 
mangraken ka-sottokaha jerangkon ja mano bolchi kaskana 
chanchiachim. Putimario Ganol chibimako dal-a bolni dolongko 
kaengachim. Mankachar aro Putimario neng-takna Rest 
Houserangko rikengahachim. 

Kakripara antidam gita jokpakgipa Dilni chibimakoba dolong 
name ka-aha. Iana skang dolong namgijagipa dongachim jeoni 
Achik sakgni anti re-bagipa ga-ake chio sripe siahachim. Ia Elekani 
Lasker nama dolongko rikchina D.C. o dabie name rikatengahachim. 
Iani koros Rs. 250/- ong genchim. Mankachar aro Kakriparani gisepo 
sorokko Garo Hillsni D.C. matchu gari re na man na gita nambate 
tariatengahachim.

Report gita, Garo Hillso 1875 ni April 1 tarikoni 1876 ni March 31

inebanikachim.

basakoba uamangni hukumna manderang aganchakon uamang bil 
gnange mesokna pa-kujachim. Uni gimin sorkari uamangni
