Gnigipa Mande So-tani : la gnigipa mande so-otani bewalara 
an-banggri mopilani ong ahachim aro ian A-chikrangni 
dingtangmanchagipa dakbewalko iano mesokahachim. Rakmanpara 
songni Thoding minggipako songtangni Kengkul minggipa saiaaro 
chonika dakahachim, indiba Kengkulko saia ba dokskani pal ua gipin 
ramakosa re-skaha. Thoding Rakmanpara songni gittimgniko soaha. 
Skanggipagitimko so-ode manderang uan maibadakesaong-ahakon 
ine chanchie dongtokengachim, indiba jensalo gnigipa gittimko 
so-taiaha, unon uamang saoba cha-u ba raseke ra-giparang dakenga 
ine chanchiskahachim. Indiba uamang ruute jajae dongna nangjaha, 
maina Thodingni kanamgija dake dena mangipa mitam manderang 
kate chong-motko agane on-aha. Skanggipa gittimko so e Thoding 
gnigipa gittimko so-eaha jeon me-chikrang aro 
bi-sarangsanmangmang dongaiachim. Ua miksongchong-moten ia 
gittimko so aha aro jensalo kenjagokgipa me-chikrang aro bi -sarang 
katbildangtokaha, unon ua uamangko den-aha. Uandake ua sak 6 
me-chikma aro bi-sarangko den-aha aro gipin sak 6 me-chik aro 
bi-sarangko mata-bu-ataha aro saksa siaha. Ua saksa me-asakoba 
mata-bu ataha. Den a man gipa pilakan sinaba dongakonchim indiba 
Turani Civil Surgeon rang-san re-ange uamangko sananichisa indake 
ong-jaha. Thoding ia dosrangko uni bobil Kengkulko dosi galatna 
aro uni gamko rusina dakaha maina A-chikni dakrongbewal gitade

pilak dos dakarangni Kengkulan daito ong achim. Uni gimin ia

nanggenchim. Indiba ia kotonako knarin D.C., Mr. Cawley aro Dr.

so-doate, B ara miterangko rebae kakketko dake on china

amgenchim aro unon A ong-gija potani gimin man-somanna dai

JATKO MA SIBACHENGANI, 1900 - 1934 

Bilsi ritchachiskuni ritchasa bilsirangni a-bachengan baksa 
Achikrang an-tangtangni dingtang jat ong-ani, samtangtangchi 
duulgipa Jomindarrangni sinchepchep-ra-chepchep dake 
a-dokrangko dokkol ka-e ra-ani aro chel-begipa a-songoni re-bae

Britishni a-songtangko dokkol ka-e nokgipa dake uamangni kosako 
sason ka-arangni gimin sa-dikako ma-sina a-bachengbaaha. A-kaweni 
manderangni chonik-onnik dakarangko aro Jomindarrangni 
sinchepchepako man-batgiparangde uamangchi an-pae 
songdonggipa Nokmarang. Sordarrang aro Laskerrang ong-achim. 
Ruuta bilsirangna skangan uamang Kachari gaae 
warachakbaengachim indiba jinma tope apsan warachaka grichim. 
Ia pilak Achikrangni neng-nikanirangko ra-gope A-chik gimikni pal 
dakgrikdilchenggipa darang gipinba ong-ja indiba uan Sonaram R. 
Sangma ong-achim. Garo Hills aro Goalpara Districtrangni 
A chikrang, mongsongbate, Salgrochipakni A chikrang. Jomindar 
aro British Sorkarini kosako Sonaram Sangmani dilanichi kachari 
gaaha aro anchi-pari nikgija dal-a movementko dakaha. Uandake 
jat gimikko ku-monge jatni namgnina dakdilanichi jattangna aro 
a-songtangna ka-sana aro gaora de-na Sonaram R. Sangma 
A chikrangni gisiko chinapataha aro British Sorkarikoba 
jingjengataha.

Bilsi 11 mangma British Sorkarini ning-o kam ka-ani 
Sonaramna bang-bea u-ie-ma sie ra anirangko on-ahachim. Uni bilsi 
10 mangna sorokni Mohori kam kamitingo ua Turaoni Rongrengona 
aro Rongrengoni Damraona songreahachim aro nokchi-jamchi 
re-pilgija ua songrangchi wenahachim. Uandaken ua bilsisamangna 
vaccinationni Inspector ong-mitingo A chik a-songni ki-tikbrichin 
wenahachim. Uni pilak wenanirango ua Achikrangni duk ong-e 
British Sorkarini kosako matnange aganani uni nachikol 
gappilachim. 

A-chikrangni mingsa dal-beamatnangniaraBegal Itani gimin 
ongachim. Ia somoerango D.C. je kamnaba okamate A-chikrangko

indin kam ka-ataiachim. Minggipinara, Garo Hillsni nambatgipa 
a dokrangko British Sorkari Reserved Forest dakna ra-sekachim. 
Indake bang-a songrangko rikgale gale aro mitamnasan nokprako 
Rs. 1 5/- dake compensation on e Reserved Forestna ra-draachim. Uni 
giminan bang-a biaprangchin aba o-e chapanan nama biaprangkon 
man-nangpiljahachim. 

Salgrochipak wenangoara Nokmarang aro Laskerrangni 
ajongrangoniko aani kajina gile chapagipa apalrangko Jomindami 
ning o napate kajinarangko gilseke ra-anirangni kattarangan 
gapchipaiachim. Iakon Nazarana ba kajina on e cha-gipa a-ani kajia 
ine mingachim. 

Salgrochipak minggipin a-ani kajiara Bijni aro Mechpara 
Jomindarrang baksa a-dokni sima-arirangni gimin ong achim. 
A chikrangni aganani gita, Garo Hillsni an-pagipa pilak 
a-kawerangon A-chikrang songdongchenge a- pal pe e hal game 
cha-chenggiparang ong-achim. Iako ma sie Moghulni chasongo 
uamangni Maharirangni skotonggipakon Sordar, Jaghirdar ba 
Laskerrang dake songe uamangkon a-brini Achikrangni sko sotna 
a-kawerangona re-bagiparangko warachakchina tik ka-e donahachim, 
Indiba Jomindarrang British Soricari baksa melie uamangni songrang 
aro game cha-gipa a-arangko Jomindarrangni ning-o survey ka-e 
napatahachim. Iako A-chikrang namnikjachim aro ruuta 
chasongrangonin bang- a A-chik Nokmarang aro Laskerrang

man-gipa maharirang kajina gri jakgitele game sue cha-na manachim 
aro uamangni Nakherajni ning-o songdonggipa Rabha, Kachan aro 
gipin a-jong-rorirangoniko kajina ra-na manachim. Kajinako tangka, 
do-o, do-gep, wak ba chuchi on-achim indiba uarangko palna aro 
bondok donna gita on-jachim. Ua maharirangan Jomindarina aro 
Britishna skangan songdongchenggiparang aro a-a 
Pruchenggiparang ong-a ine nike Nakherajko see on-ahachim.

mongsonggipa a-sel ong-jachim, maina 1867 bilsio Garo Hills 
Districtko dakon ua Districtni daito ong-aha. Uni giminan 1877 bilsio

ga-akachim. 28

Nokma, Laskerrangni reange uamangko dakdilchina 
mol-molako knachake Sonaram jatko dilna aro asongko chelchakna 
gita miksongaha. Uni jatna aro a-songna kam ka-na rechakataniko 
uni 1902 ni February 16 tariko agan-parakgipaoniko u ina man-a. 
Ua aganaha. "Bijnini kosako case ka-giparangni lekkarangko anga 
man-rongenga. Iarangko anga batanggipa jasnimangna 
chimongbaengachim. Iarangko chimonge anga da-alona Rs. 700/- 
mang koros ong-aha. Anga ukilrangko Goalparao grongeaha. Bilsi 
100 na skang A chikrang Nazarana a dokrangni kosako bil 
gnangchim aro ua Mahalrangko chengonin man-achim. Indiba da-o 
iarangko anchingoniko ra-e katangaha. Ian 1886-87 bilsirango Mr. 
Heathni Garo Hillsni D.C. ong-miting somoirango ongachim. Uni 
ja-mano uni A chikrang British Sorkarina kajina on-na nangaha. 
Kajinarangko gile Treasuryo donaha ine knasoaha. Uan gimik Rs. 
60,000/- ongachim. Ua adokrangko British Sorkari dokkol ka-ani

Nakherajrangko dabipilani gimin janapaha. Uko chinga dabipila 
maina chinga iani bidingo changsaba see Chief Commissionemi 
mikkangona re-anga dongkujachim. la chong-mot ong-aniko 
dabianichi golmal-gondokol ong-atenga ine inna manjawa maina 
pil-sa gitara anga Assamoni chela biapoba dongenga. Anga golmal- 
gondokolko ong-atna miksonga dongja aro uko chanchiaba dongja. 
Angni dabiani chong-mot onga aro anga Sorkarina bebe ong-gipa 
onga indiba Bijni Jomindami A-chikrangni a dokrangko sekdrae 
ra-ahani giminsa anga iarangko dakenga. Sorkari aro uni officerang 
iako tik bichal ka-e on-na bil dongani giminsa iarangko Chief 
Commissionemi bichalona ra-bae on-enga. Uni gimin angni daka 
kamrangko Sorkariko bobil dinge dakenga ine chanchina man jawa. 
Uni gimin ia chong-motgipa miksonga gnang dakaniko Chief 
(Commissionerna agane onpachina molmola. 

Angni patoko dongtaianiko nikna Chief Commissioner duk 
onggen inako knae anga uko mittela aro ango golmal-gondokolko 
dakna skani dongja ine anga una aganna ska. Anga golmal dakako

mitchia aro anga Sorkarina bame kam kana ska aro uani giminsa 
anga niam gita, kakket ong-e aro tom-toma bewalchi bichal ka-e 
on-aniko Sorkario mol mola. Angade jinmani pal see mol-molnasa, 
mamingsaloba golmal ka na ong-ja. Tangka gile ra-naba angan 
bon kamgipa mande ong gen. Anga tangka gilama gilja, angni jako 
donama donja, aro ango on*eama on-eja na an sandie nina man-gen. 
Anga angni kakket ong-e man-aon pangchake janggi tanga aro gipinni 
on-gilarango pangchake janggi tangna an-tangkon one nika. 
Nokmarangni Anna bri kajinako ra-na jechakako angni a-sel ong-ama 
ong-ja na-an sandie nibo. Uade angni bobilrangni jangatasan ong-aia 
aro maming kakket ong-ja aro ong-naba man-ja. Anga nang-ni 
bichalna pangnan senga indiba kam nangchongmotani gimin anga 
Calcuttaon dongna nangkuenga. "Na-a Mr. Fullema chol man-on 
indake agane on-gen ine anga ka donga ." 66

RESERVED FORESTRANGNA DAKGRIKANI 

1 879 bilsion Garo Hillsni buringrangko Reserve Forest dakna 
chanchina a-bachengaha. 1881 bilsio Assistant Conservator of Forest, 
Mr. Fisherko Garo Hillschi re bae niechina watataha. Mr. Fisher 
re jojoe nie Garo Hillsni buringrangko Reserve Forest dakna nanga 
ine British Sorkariko didiaha. Uni ku pattiani gita dam 16 
Forestrangko 1883 aro 1885 bilsirangni gisepo Reserve Forestrang 
dakaha aro 1887-1888ni gisepo Baghmara aro Angratoliko Reserved 
Forest dakataha. Reserved Forestrangko dakon bang a songrangko 
jitatna nangaha. Ua jitata mangipa songrangna ba nokrangna 
maming gipin cholrangko dake on-skaa dongjachim. Badiaba 
songrangni adita nokdangrangnasan nokprako Rs. 15/- kae 
compensation on-aiachim. 79 A-rika man gipa pilakan ong-nasipile 
jakari gipin biaprangchi kate dongna nangaiachim. Reserved 
ka-gimin buringrangoniko maming teng sakoba aro maming do-o, 
mat aro matburingkoba den-na ba so-otna on-jachim. Uni gimin 
Sonaramni P.W.D.ni Mohori ong e songrangchi wenroromitingo

Sonaram bang-a A-chikrangni duk ong-e agananiko knaahachim. 
Uni giminsa Sonaram A-dokna Dakgrikan baksa ia reserved 
Forestrangko A-chikrangna on-pilna nanga, ong-jaode uamangna 
kraa compensationko on-na nanga ine Memorandumrangko 
onahachim. 80 ■■

BEGAL ITANI GIMIN DAKGRIKANI 


Begal ita nokkol baksa apsanaiachim. Ita man-giparang indin 
dangdike on-na nangaiachim. On-paosa ra-na man-aiachim. 
Britishrang Garo Hillsko dokkol ka-on Achikrangko Begal itna 
a-bachengaha. British sipairang Garo Hillschi chagropangon

A chikrangko merong ba gipin bosturangko olattimatachim. 
Officerrangba songrangchi songreango an-tangtangni mal- 
bosturangko indin olatrongachim. Forest Guardrangba indin 
buringrangko o-atrongachim. Uandaken songni manderangkon 
rama-sorok cho-atronga are sorkarini Inspection Bungalowrangko 
riko kam kaatachim. Nokmarangkoba kulirangko watatchina hukum 
on-atrongachim. Uandake ia namen namjabegipa bewalko Garo 
Hillso ragataha. A-chikrang ia pilak sinjetanirangna mamingkoba 
dakna u-ijae jrip jrip chakchikbaaha. Sonaram indin nigake dongna 
man-jaha. Uni gimin ua uni Nazarana. Nakheraj aro Habraghat 
a-dokrangna dakgrikan baksa ia Begal itani bewalkoba gimatna 
nanga ine memorandumrangko see dabiaha. 

Garo Hillsoniko Begal Itani bewalko gimatchina ine 
Sonaram 1 905 ni August 23 tariko memorandum see Assam ni Chief 
Commissionema on-chengaha. Begal itani ba Drae indin kam 
ka-atani bewalko gimaatchina dabiani Achikrangoniko tangka gilani 
gimin uamangna mesokna dakmikani ong-ja indiba uan 
mongsonggipa A chikrangni saknabegipa ong chongmota aro uan 
sorkarini jakchi namataniko senge dongenga. 84 1905ni November 
23 tariko Indiani Viceroyna memorandum on-gipaoba Begal Itako 
gimatchina mol-moltaiaha. Ianojanapani gita, A-chikrangni gnigipa 
duk- saknaaniar a Begal Itani bewal ong-a jean Garo Hillso ruuta 
bilsirangna dakbaaha. A-chikrang namnikbo ba namnikjabo, 
Sorkarini Office rrang uamangko drae kam ka-atronga. Basakoba 
banggija tangka on-a, basakobade on-srangja indiba officemi hukum

2. Habraghat Pargana : 1902ni December jao Sonaramni

A-chikrangko Damraoni Dolgumaona re-jomdilangaoni Sonaram 
Habraghat Parganako A-chikrangni a-dok ine dabina a-bachengaha. 
Habraghat Parganara Garo Hillsni Salgrochi Songduona, Salaramchi 
Dhupdhora gita Songduona aro Saliramchi Paikan gita Songdu 
nadike Pargana ba damsa sasonna bak dakgipa ong-achim aro uni 
apala 500 square milerang ong-naba dongachim. Bijni Ranini 1905 
bilsio Salpara-Roumari songni Sonaramko man-chape sak 12 game 
cha-giparangni kosako kajina on-jani bidingo case ka-ako 1 906 bilsio 
bichal ka-o jera ra-miting somoiosa Sonaram uni jikgipa Thokje G. 
Mominni bimingo Habraghat parganako uamangnisa ine 
dabichengaha. Dorkastoode Sonaram an-tang Bijni a-ako game 
cha-gipa inejanapachim. Uni gimin Bijni Raniara kajinako uagame 
cha-giparangoniko gilgenma, ma Sonaraman uamangni skotong 
ong a ine Sonaramko gilate unonikosa dabigenma. Iakosa jera 
ra-engachim indiba 1906ni April 4 tariko Calcutta Bami ukil Mr. 
Jacob sokbaon, Sonaram ma-ang Bijnini a-ako game cha-giparang 
ong-ja ine jeaha. Ua mingsinggipa ukil indake jeaha je, Sonaram 
Sangma, uni jikgipani pal Rohumari jol gimikni mangmang ong-ja 
indiba ua Habraghat Pargana gimiknin nokgipa ong-a. 1903 bilsio 
an-tangko Criminal Caseo galani gimin Kachario dakgrikmiting 
somoiode Sonaram an-tangko Baro laskerrangni paisa dakgrikenga 
aro mitam a-dokni bidingode Bijnini ning-o ong-a ine aganachim 
indiba 1906 ni June 16 tariko, Habraghat Pargana uni 
jikgipamanchani ong-a, gipin Nokmarangde Nazarana Mahalrangni 
nokgiparangsan ong-aia ine janapskaaha. An-tangni dabianiko

bilsio chanchina a-bachengaha. Mr. Fisher, Assistant Conservator

jekon Garo Hillsni buringgrimrangko sandiechina watatahachim ua

Mr. Arbuthnottni report gita 1883 bilsio Reserved Forest 
dakgiparang - Dhima, Dilma, Rajasimla, Ildek, Darengiri, Chima- 
Bangshi, Rongrenggiri, Siju, Rewak, Emangiri, Dibri Hill, Bolsalgiri, 
Tura aro Jinari. 1885 bilsio Dambu aro Songsako Reserved Forest 
dakaha. 1887 bilsio Baghmara aro 1888 bilsio Angratoliko Reserved 
Forest dakaha. Simsang chibimani a-kawerang gita Assam Railway 
Lineko dakna miksonga dongani gimin Darugiri aro Dambu 
Reserved Forestko ra-pretangnaba donga ine Sorkari chanchiaha. 

Reserved Forest dakna nokrangko jitgalatna nokprako Rs. 
15/- ko tik kaahachim indiba bang gija songrangni nokrangsan 
tangkako man aiahachim. Uandaken Chima-Bangshi Reserved 
Forestna nok 6 nasan Rs. 15/- ka-e nokprakna compensation on aha, 
Dhimana nok 6 na on aha. Bangki songrangna on-jaha. Uni gimin 
indake maming compensation gri skatang song-nokrangko rikgale 
gale Forest Reservationrangko dakaniba ia golmalni mingsa 
dal-begipa a-sel ong-a ine nikaha. 

Aroba 1900 bilsio Chima-Bangshi aro Dhima 
Reserverangko bariatna janapaniba A-chikrangko 
dongs iksakat bataha maina apsan bilsion Bijni Jomindar Survey ka-e 
golmal ong atengachim. Ia diltubegipa biaprangni A-chikrang 
Habraghat Parganani gimin gisik nangja indiba Reserved Forest aro 
Begar systemni gimin gisik nangachim aro unasa tangkakoba 
gilatachim. Reserved Forestni rakgipa niamrangnaba A-chikrang 
namnikjachim maina Reserved Forestoniko uamang manungko 
den-na, na-tok rim-na, sikar ka-na, Bija bitchi am-na aro biterangko 
chimongna man-jachim. Aroba a-ba o-ako so-e Reserved Forestchi 
wa-al sasti on-achim. Iarangna bateba A-chikrang Reserved 

Forestrangko a-ba cha-na nangnikbatachim. Uni gimin uamangni

milcs nng A manr haHft uarangko man-pilnasa ong-achim. Minggipin,

Achik jat gimik ongnasipile “Baro Lasker”ni bimingo Sonaram 
R. Sangma dakgrikdilbaoba uoni pangkamgipa kumongrimani dol 
nakatbajaha. Uandake ia chasongona kingking Achik jatni gisepo 
toromni gita A chik Baptist Dalgipa Krimana agre darang gipin 
nangrimgrike kamkaani Dol gripitichim. Badiaba jolni manderangna 
maiba sinjetani sokbaosa ua jolni manderangni gisepo maiba 
nangrimgrikani Dolko dake dabianirangko dakaiachim. Indakgipa 
uamangni nanganina dabina gita Goalpara Districtni Achikrangna 
sokbaaha. 1914 bilsio Assam Sorkarini Director of Public 
Instructions indake geetaha, “Goalpara Districtni ningo badita 
A chik Schoolrang donga, uarangko skia aro nirokani balko Garo 
Hillsni ning o dongija Goalparani ning o donatskagen.” Ian 
Goalparani A chik Schoolrangna namjawa maina Goalpara Local 
Boardni jakosa ong gnok aro unode maming nirok-simsakaniko 
manjawaha ine nikaha. Uni gimin Garo Hills baksan apsan skiani 
aro nirokani ongtaipilchina aro Salgro jolni bijangchio M.E. School 
ge-sa dake on<pachina 1915 ni February 26 - 27 tarikrango 
Nishangram songo “Garo National Congregation” ine Dolni 
bimingko done tom-aha, aro dabianirangko janape D.P.I. na dorkasto 
see onaha. Ia Dolni Chairman Tuniram R. Marak, Vice-Chairman, 
Kosan G. Momin aro gipin memborrang, Gopal G. Momin, Atting 
K. Sangma aro Singgan D. Shira ongachim. 1 Ian Baro Laskemi 
ja mano Achikrangni nangrimgrikani Dol ong achim.

Mahatma Gandhi South Africaoni Indiaona re bapilosa 
abachengaha. Uandake Mahatma Gandhini didianichi 1920 ni Sept, 
jao Calcuttao Congressni Special Sessionko ong-ate Non- 
Cooperation Movementko dakna tik ka aha. Ia non-Copperation 
somoio, 1920 - 22, Indiani manderang an tangtangni British 
Sorkarioniko mangimin upati aro Medalrangko Sorkarina onpilgen,

gelgen aro bon kamao Sorkarina kajina on aniko dontonggen.* 5 Ia 
dal-begipa Non-Cooperation Movemento Achikrangba nikuake 
dongaijaha, Garo Hills-Goalpara sima jolrango donggipa mitam 
A chikrangba jak ra paaha. Uamangoni saksa Thorka songoni 
Kiboram Garo aro sakgipinara Subarkona songoni Rabiram Garo 
ong achim. Iamang 1922 bilsio Non-Cooperation Movemento jak

ra-pae Police Lock-Up o donako man aha.

dongpaenggenkonchim. Indiba 1942 bilsio Quit India Movementode

To, 

The Chairman, 

British Parliamentary Delegation, 

Dated Tura the 25th February, 1945. 

Sir, 

We, the people of the Garo Hills District are filled with 
dismay to hear the rumour that there is a plan of some British officers 
in Assam to exclude our district from Assam and India and put in 
within an excluded area which may be called the North-Eastern 
British Protectorate which we presume will be a Non-Regulated 
and to be ruled by political officials.

We oppose the plan. It is undesirable that we should be 
included in that kind of Protectorate, but it is desired that we should 
be with Assam and India. 


Yours faithfully 

1 . Emonsing M. Sangma 

2. Gauram R. Marak 

3. Singdon K. Sangma 33
